class BaselineCorrectionRun(Base):
    """Independent correction analysis for one frozen baseline run."""

    __tablename__ = "baseline_correction_runs"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("idempotency_key", name="uq_baseline_correction_key"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('processing','awaiting_decision','approved','rejected','failed')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "stage IN ('analysis','candidate_generation','candidate_validation','regression','decision')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_stage",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "decision IS NULL OR decision IN ('approved','rejected')",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_decision",
        ),
        CheckConstraint("attempt_count >= 1", name="ck_baseline_correction_attempts"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "progress BETWEEN 0 AND 100",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_progress",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(selected_item_ids_json) AND "
            "json_type(selected_item_ids_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_selected_items",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(input_snapshot_json) AND "
            "json_type(input_snapshot_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_input",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(report_json) AND json_type(report_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_report",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(blockers_json) AND json_type(blockers_json, '$') = 'array'",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_blockers",
        ),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), unique=True, index=True)
    baseline_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True
    )
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    selected_item_ids_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    input_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="processing", index=True)
    stage: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="analysis", index=True)
    progress: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    report_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    blockers_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="[]")
    orchestration_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}")
    candidate_revision_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("category_evaluation_v3_revisions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("workflow_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    regression_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("baseline_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="")
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    decision: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    decided_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    decided_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )
    decision_note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow, onupdate=utcnow
    )
    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True
    )

    baseline_run: Mapped[BaselineRegressionRun] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[baseline_run_id]
    )


class BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate(Base):
    """One immutable component replacement considered by a correction run."""

    __tablename__ = "baseline_correction_component_candidates"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(

            "node_instance_id",

        ),

            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_selection",
        ),

            "(source_prompt_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND candidate_prompt_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND source_rule_module_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND candidate_rule_module_version_id IS NULL) OR "
            "(source_prompt_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND candidate_prompt_version_id IS NULL "
            "AND source_rule_module_version_id IS NOT NULL "
            "AND candidate_rule_module_version_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_version_pair",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(content_hash) = 64",
            name="ck_baseline_correction_component_hash",
        ),
    )

    )

        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_prompt_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("prompt_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    source_rule_module_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("rule_module_versions.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        nullable=True,
        index=True,
    )
    candidate_rule_module_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(

    )
    selection_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), index=True)

        Text, default="{}", server_default="{}"
    )
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), default="system")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow
    )

    correction_run: Mapped[BaselineCorrectionRun] = relationship()


class BaselineFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a frozen baseline definition is changed in place."""


@event.listens_for(BaselineSet, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineSet, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_set_mutation(
    _mapper: object, _connection: Connection, _target: BaselineSet
) -> None:
    raise BaselineFrozenError("BaselineSet 创建后不可修改或删除")


@event.listens_for(BaselineSetItem, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineSetItem, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_set_item_mutation(
    _mapper: object, _connection: Connection, _target: BaselineSetItem
) -> None:
    raise BaselineFrozenError("BaselineSet 条目创建后不可修改或删除")


class BaselineTruthFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when append-only baseline truth is changed in place."""


@event.listens_for(BaselineTruthRevision, "before_update")
@event.listens_for(BaselineTruthRevision, "before_delete")
def _prevent_baseline_truth_revision_mutation(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    _target: BaselineTruthRevision,
) -> None:
    raise BaselineTruthFrozenError("BaselineTruthRevision 不可修改或删除")

    completed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)

    prompt_b: Mapped[PromptVersion] = relationship(foreign_keys=[prompt_b_id])
    baseline_strategy_bundle: Mapped[StrategyBundle | None] = relationship(
        foreign_keys=[baseline_strategy_bundle_id]
    )

        foreign_keys=[candidate_strategy_bundle_id]
    )
    items: Mapped[list["PromptRegressionItem"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="run", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="PromptRegressionItem.id"

class EvaluationPackage(Base):
    """Immutable review and release aggregate for one candidate strategy."""

    __tablename__ = "evaluation_packages"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "package_key", name="uq_evaluation_packages_package_key"
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ("
            "'validating','awaiting_review','approved','rejected',"
            "'published','archived'"
            ")",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_status",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "prompt_mode IN ('single','dual')",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_prompt_mode",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "(prompt_mode = 'single' AND prompt_b_id IS NULL) OR "
            "(prompt_mode = 'dual' AND prompt_b_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_prompt_mode_refs",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "json_valid(canonical_manifest_json) AND "
            "json_type(canonical_manifest_json, '$') = 'object'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_manifest_json",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(canonical_manifest_hash) = 64 AND "
            "canonical_manifest_hash = lower(canonical_manifest_hash) AND "
            "canonical_manifest_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_manifest_hash",
        ),
        CheckConstraint(
            "length(request_hash) = 64 AND "
            "request_hash = lower(request_hash) AND "
            "request_hash NOT GLOB '*[^0-9a-f]*'",
            name="ck_evaluation_packages_request_hash",

            "review_revision >= 0",

class EvaluationPackageFrozenError(ValueError):
    """Raised when frozen package identity or reviewed evidence is changed."""


_EVALUATION_PACKAGE_IDENTITY_FIELDS = (
    "package_key",
    "request_hash",
    "category_key",
    "prompt_mode",
    "prompt_a_id",
    "prompt_b_id",
    "dimension_schema_id",
    "dimension_route_policy_id",
    "sample_set_id",
    "baseline_strategy_bundle_id",
    "candidate_strategy_bundle_id",
    "regression_run_id",
    "automation_run_id",
    "metric_snapshot_id",
)


@event.listens_for(EvaluationPackage, "before_update")
def _protect_evaluation_package_update(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    target: EvaluationPackage,
) -> None:
    changed_identity = _changed_fields(
        target, _EVALUATION_PACKAGE_IDENTITY_FIELDS
    )
    if changed_identity:
        raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
            "EvaluationPackage 冻结引用禁止修改："
            + "、".join(sorted(changed_identity))
        )
    manifest_changed = _changed_fields(
        target,
        (
            "canonical_manifest_json",
            "canonical_manifest_hash",
            "ai_recommendation",
            "change_summary",
        ),
    )
    status_history = attributes.get_history(target, "status")
    previous_status = (
        str(status_history.deleted[0])
        if status_history.deleted
        else target.status
    )
    if manifest_changed and previous_status != "validating":
        raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
            "EvaluationPackage 进入二审后冻结清单禁止修改"
        )


@event.listens_for(EvaluationPackage, "before_delete")
def _protect_evaluation_package_delete(
    _mapper: object,
    _connection: Connection,
    _target: EvaluationPackage,
) -> None:
    raise EvaluationPackageFrozenError(
        "EvaluationPackage 是永久审计记录，禁止删除"
    )

===== FILE backend/app/module_api.py =====
"""Explicit API deployment ownership, also used to generate nginx locations."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

PREFIXES = {
    "incremental": (
        "incremental-mechanisms", "incremental-materials", "incremental-evidence",
        "incremental-corrections", "incremental-correction-analysis", "failures",
        "source-recovery", "connectors", "connector-governance", "content-ingress",
        "incremental-automation", "incremental-dashboard", "incremental-controls",
    ),
    "stock": (
        "baseline-sets", "baseline-regressions", "baseline-truth-contracts",
        "baseline-corrections", "baseline-exports", "prompt-regressions",
        "paired-regressions", "sample-sets", "design-case-samples",
        "design-case-dimension-samples", "dimension-schemas", "dimension-route-policies",
    ),
}
MODULES = ("common", "incremental", "stock")
SHARED_PREFIXES = ("/api/auth/", "/api/health/")


def owner(path: str) -> str:
    head = path.split("/")[2] if path.startswith("/api/") else ""
    for module, prefixes in PREFIXES.items():
        if head in prefixes:
            return module
    return "common"


def serves(module: str, path: str) -> bool:
    return owner(path) == module or path.startswith(SHARED_PREFIXES)


class InstanceActivity:
    """Observe in-flight HTTP, keep draining independent of fleet readiness."""
    def __init__(self, app, *, module: str, drain_file: Path):
        self.app, self.module, self.drain_file = app, module, drain_file
        self.active = 0

    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
        if scope["type"] != "http":
            return await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        path = scope["path"]
        if path == "/api/health/module":
            return await JSONResponse({"module": self.module, "active_requests": self.active,
                "draining": self.drain_file.exists(), "build_sha": os.getenv("LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA", "unknown")})(scope, receive, send)
        # Gate the resolved request path, not app.router.routes: recent FastAPI
        # versions retain include_router() as lazy containers without .path.
        # Filtering those containers silently removes all included business APIs.
        # Leave FastAPI's router/dependencies intact and reject other modules
        # before any handler or dependency runs.
        if not serves(self.module, path):
            return await JSONResponse({"detail": "Not Found"}, status_code=404)(scope, receive, send)
        if self.drain_file.exists():
            return await JSONResponse({"code": "instance_draining"}, status_code=503)(scope, receive, send)
        self.active += 1
        try:
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        finally:
            self.active -= 1


def create_app():
    from .main import app
    module = os.environ.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE", "common")
    if module not in MODULES:
        raise ValueError("invalid LABEL_SYSTEM_API_MODULE")
    app.add_middleware(InstanceActivity, module=module,
        drain_file=Path(os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_API_DRAIN_FILE", "/tmp/label-api-draining")))
    return app

===== FILE backend/app/module_runtime.py =====
"""Same-host business execution boundaries; shared quota/ledger remain authoritative.

The database is the source of admission limits, never one limit per process.
All control changes and claims serialize on the existing scheduler row. A drain
stops new claims only: it does not mutate runs, leases, checkpoints or requests.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import ModuleRuntimeState, ProductionRun, RuntimeDispatchItem, ProductionStepAttempt, EvaluationJob, AutomationWorkerStatus

POOLS = ("incremental", "stock", "compatibility")
EXECUTOR_REVISION = "runtime-v1"
SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS = (EXECUTOR_REVISION,)


def execution_pool(source_type: str | None, run_mode: str | None) -> str:
    if source_type in {"content_ingress_event", "upstream_intake_event"} and run_mode == "incremental":
        return "incremental"
    if source_type in {"baseline_regression_item", "baseline_regression"} and run_mode in {None, "stock_regression"}:
        return "stock"
    # Missing/contradictory provenance is visible and executable in its own pool.
    return "compatibility"


def configured_pool() -> str | None:
    value = os.environ.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_EXECUTION_POOL", "")
    if value and value not in POOLS:
        raise ValueError("invalid LABEL_SYSTEM_EXECUTION_POOL")
    return value or None


def states(db: Session) -> list[ModuleRuntimeState]:
    return list(db.scalars(select(ModuleRuntimeState).order_by(ModuleRuntimeState.pool)))


def configuration(db: Session) -> dict[str, ModuleRuntimeState]:
    rows = {row.pool: row for row in states(db)}
    if not rows:
        return {}
    if set(rows) != set(POOLS):
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime requires all three pools")
    limits = {row.global_limit for row in rows.values()}
    if len(limits) != 1 or any(row.capacity < 1 for row in rows.values()):
        raise RuntimeError("invalid module runtime capacities")
    if sum(row.capacity for row in rows.values()) > next(iter(limits)):
        raise RuntimeError("module pool capacities exceed global limit")
    return rows


def runtime_predicate(pool: str):
    if pool not in POOLS:
        raise ValueError("invalid execution pool")
    return (
        ProductionRun.execution_pool == pool,
        ProductionRun.executor_revision.in_(SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS),
    )


def leased_counts(db: Session) -> dict[str, int]:
    counts = {pool: 0 for pool in POOLS}
    for pool, count in db.execute(
        select(ProductionRun.execution_pool, func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id))
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == ProductionRun.id)
        .join(RuntimeDispatchItem, RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == ProductionStepAttempt.id)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")
        .group_by(ProductionRun.execution_pool)
    ):
        # Unknown pool values consume global capacity and fail closed.
        counts[pool] = int(count)
    counts["compatibility"] += int(db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id)).where(
        EvaluationJob.status == "processing")) or 0)
    return counts


def control(db: Session, *, pool: str, release_id: str, action: str) -> dict:
    from .runtime_queue import _scheduler_state
    from .queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy
    if pool not in POOLS or action not in {"drain", "resume"}:
        raise ValueError("invalid module control action")
    if not release_id or len(release_id) > 80:
        raise ValueError("release_id required (max 80 chars)")
    rows = configuration(db)
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime is not configured")
    _scheduler_state(db, QueuePolicy(global_limit=rows[pool].global_limit))
    row = db.scalar(select(ModuleRuntimeState).where(ModuleRuntimeState.pool == pool)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if action == "drain":
        if row.release_id and row.release_id != release_id:
            raise RuntimeError("module is owned by another release")
        row.accepting = False
        row.release_id = release_id
    else:
        if row.release_id != release_id:
            raise RuntimeError("resume requires the owning release_id")
        row.accepting = True
        row.release_id = ""
    db.flush()
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db, category="module_release", action=action,
        subject_type="execution_pool", subject_id=pool, actor="release:" + release_id,
        payload={"release_id": release_id, "accepting": row.accepting})
    return {"pool": pool, "accepting": row.accepting, "release_id": row.release_id,
            "leased": leased_counts(db)[pool]}


def status(db: Session) -> dict:
    rows = configuration(db)
    counts = leased_counts(db)
    unsupported = list(db.execute(select(ProductionRun.execution_pool, ProductionRun.executor_revision,
        func.count()).where(ProductionRun.status.in_(("queued", "running", "retryable")),
        ProductionRun.executor_revision.not_in(SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS))
        .group_by(ProductionRun.execution_pool, ProductionRun.executor_revision)))
    workers=[]
    for worker in db.scalars(select(AutomationWorkerStatus).where(
            AutomationWorkerStatus.last_seen_at >= datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(seconds=45))):
        result=json.loads(worker.last_result_json or '{}')
        if result.get('execution_pool') in POOLS:
            workers.append({'worker_id':worker.worker_id,'pool':result['execution_pool'],
                'build_sha':result.get('build_sha'),'readiness':worker.readiness,
                'started_at':worker.started_at.replace(tzinfo=worker.started_at.tzinfo or timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                'active':result.get('active'), 'last_status':worker.last_status})
    return {"configured": bool(rows), "supported_executor_revisions": SUPPORTED_EXECUTOR_REVISIONS,
        "workers":workers,
        "pools": [{"pool": pool, "capacity": row.capacity, "global_limit": row.global_limit,
                   "accepting": row.accepting, "release_id": row.release_id, "leased": counts[pool]}
                  for pool, row in rows.items()],
        "unsupported": [{"pool": p, "revision": r, "count": n} for p, r, n in unsupported]}


def initialize(db: Session, capacities: dict[str, int]) -> None:
    """One-time offline cutover; cannot silently overwrite a live configuration."""
    if set(capacities) != set(POOLS) or any(type(n) is not int or n < 1 for n in capacities.values()):
        raise ValueError("three positive integer capacities required")
    if states(db):
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime already initialized")
    if any(leased_counts(db).values()):
        raise RuntimeError("initial cutover requires all legacy executors drained")
    limit = sum(capacities.values())
    db.add_all([ModuleRuntimeState(pool=pool, capacity=n, global_limit=limit, accepting=True,
                                  release_id="") for pool, n in capacities.items()])
    db.flush()


def resize(db: Session, capacities: dict[str, int], *, actor: str = "operator:module_runtime") -> dict:
    """Online capacity change; the next claim reads the new rows under the scheduler lock.

    Growing is always safe. Shrinking is refused while a pool holds more leases
    than its new capacity (nothing is preempted). Refused while any pool is
    owned by a release, so a release window never races a capacity change.
    """
    if set(capacities) != set(POOLS) or any(type(n) is not int or n < 1 for n in capacities.values()):
        raise ValueError("three positive integer capacities required")
    from .runtime_queue import _scheduler_state
    from .queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy
    rows = configuration(db)
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError("module runtime is not configured")
    # Same lock order as control()/claims: scheduler row first, then the pool rows.
    _scheduler_state(db, QueuePolicy(global_limit=next(iter(rows.values())).global_limit))
    rows = {row.pool: row for row in db.scalars(select(ModuleRuntimeState).order_by(ModuleRuntimeState.pool)
                                                .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))}
    owned = sorted(pool for pool, row in rows.items() if row.release_id)
    if owned:
        raise RuntimeError("resize refused: pools owned by a release: " + ", ".join(owned))
    counts = leased_counts(db)
    for pool in POOLS:
        if capacities[pool] < rows[pool].capacity and counts[pool] > capacities[pool]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"resize refused: pool {pool} has {counts[pool]} leased above new capacity {capacities[pool]}")
    before = {pool: {"capacity": rows[pool].capacity, "global_limit": rows[pool].global_limit} for pool in POOLS}
    limit = sum(capacities.values())
    for pool in POOLS:
        rows[pool].capacity = capacities[pool]
        rows[pool].global_limit = limit
    db.flush()
    after = {pool: {"capacity": capacities[pool], "global_limit": limit} for pool in POOLS}
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db, category="module_runtime", action="resize",
        subject_type="module_runtime", subject_id="pools", actor=actor,
        payload={"before": before, "after": after, "leased": counts})
    return {"before": before, "after": after, "leased": counts}


def main() -> None:
    import argparse
    from .database import session_scope
    from .service_entry import validate_database
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("action", choices=("status", "drain", "resume", "initialize", "resize"))
    parser.add_argument("--pool", choices=POOLS)
    parser.add_argument("--release-id")
    parser.add_argument("--capacities", help="JSON with three pool capacities")
    parser.add_argument("--offline-confirmed", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--actor", default="operator:module_runtime", help="audit actor for resize")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    validate_database()
    with session_scope() as db:
        if args.action == "initialize":
            if not args.offline_confirmed:
                parser.error("initialize requires --offline-confirmed")
            initialize(db, json.loads(args.capacities or "{}"))
        elif args.action == "resize":
            if not args.capacities:
                parser.error("resize requires --capacities")
            resize(db, json.loads(args.capacities), actor=args.actor)
        elif args.action != "status":
            control(db, pool=args.pool, release_id=args.release_id, action=args.action)
        print(json.dumps(status(db), ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== FILE backend/app/mysql_importer.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    Connection,
    DateTime,
    Engine,
    Integer,
    Table,
    and_,
    func,
    inspect,
    select,
    text,
)
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401
from .database import Base
from .invariant_catalog import INVARIANT_CATALOG
from .migrations.mysql_invariants import (
    MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS,
    install_mysql_invariants,
    installed_mysql_invariants,
)
from .snapshot_import_common import (
    ImportPlan,
    ImportReport,
    InjectedImportStop,
    SnapshotImportError,
    load_checkpoint,
    open_immutable_sqlite,
    resolve_source_snapshot,
    source_batch,
    source_schema_compatibility_version,
    validate_source_schema,
    verify_source_unchanged,
    write_checkpoint,
)


MySQLImportError = SnapshotImportError


def _mysql_identifier(value: str) -> str:
    if len(value) > 64 or re.fullmatch(r"[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*", value) is None:
        raise MySQLImportError(f"不安全的 MySQL 标识符：{value}")
    return f"`{value}`"


def _target_schema(connection: Connection) -> str:
    value = connection.scalar(text("SELECT DATABASE()"))
    schema = "" if value is None else str(value).strip()
    if (
        len(schema) > 64
        or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", schema) is None
    ):
        raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 导入目标 schema 名称不安全")
    return schema


def assert_mysql_import_schema_empty(connection: Connection) -> None:
    """Refuse to initialize an import target that already contains objects."""

    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    inspector = inspect(connection)
    existing = sorted(
        set(inspector.get_table_names()) | set(inspector.get_view_names())
    )
    if existing:
        raise MySQLImportError(
            "MySQL 探针目标不是全空 schema，禁止初始化或覆盖"
        )


def _assert_target_ready(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    resumed: bool,
) -> None:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    actual_tables = set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
    missing = set(Base.metadata.tables) - actual_tables
    if missing:
        raise MySQLImportError(f"MySQL 目标缺少基线表：{sorted(missing)}")
    progress = checkpoint["tables"]
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        actual_count = int(
            connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
        )
        if not resumed and actual_count:
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 目标表 {table.name} 非空，禁止首次合并导入"
            )
        if resumed and table.name in progress:
            recorded_count = int(progress[table.name]["imported_rows"])
            if actual_count < recorded_count:
                raise MySQLImportError(
                    f"MySQL 目标表 {table.name} 少于检查点记录"
                )


def _comparison_value(value: Any, table: Table, column_name: str) -> Any:
    column = table.c[column_name]
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(column.type, Boolean):
        return bool(value)
    if isinstance(column.type, DateTime) and isinstance(value, datetime):
        if value.tzinfo is not None:
            value = value.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
        return value
    return value


def _rows_match(
    existing: dict[str, Any],
    incoming: dict[str, Any],
    table: Table,
) -> bool:
    return all(
        _comparison_value(existing[column_name], table, column_name)
        == _comparison_value(value, table, column_name)
        for column_name, value in incoming.items()
    )


def _insert_batch(
    connection: Connection,
    table: Table,
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
    primary_keys = [row[primary_key.name] for row in rows]
    existing_rows = connection.execute(
        select(table).where(primary_key.in_(primary_keys))
    ).mappings().all()
    existing_by_key = {
        row[primary_key.name]: dict(row)
        for row in existing_rows
    }
    missing_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    duplicate_rows = 0
    for row in rows:
        existing = existing_by_key.get(row[primary_key.name])
        if existing is None:
            missing_rows.append(row)
            continue
        if not _rows_match(existing, row, table):
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 导入表 {table.name} 发现同主键冲突；行内容已隐藏"
            )
        duplicate_rows += 1
    if missing_rows:
        connection.execute(table.insert(), missing_rows)
    return len(missing_rows), duplicate_rows


def _verify_imported_row_counts(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    plan: ImportPlan,
) -> None:
    for table_name in plan:
        table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
        expected = int(checkpoint["tables"][table_name]["imported_rows"])
        actual = int(
            connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
        )
        if actual != expected:
            raise MySQLImportError(
                f"MySQL 导入表 {table_name} 行数与源快照不一致"
            )


def _verify_no_foreign_key_orphans(connection: Connection) -> None:
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for ordinal, constraint in enumerate(table.foreign_key_constraints):
            elements = tuple(constraint.elements)
            if not elements:
                continue
            parent_table = elements[0].column.table
            parent = parent_table.alias(
                f"fk_parent_{table.name}_{ordinal}"
            )
            join_condition = and_(
                *(
                    element.parent
                    == parent.c[element.column.name]
                    for element in elements
                )
            )
            local_values_present = and_(
                *(element.parent.is_not(None) for element in elements)
            )
            missing_parent = parent.c[elements[0].column.name].is_(None)
            orphan_count = int(
                connection.scalar(
                    select(func.count())
                    .select_from(table.outerjoin(parent, join_condition))
                    .where(local_values_present, missing_parent)
                )
                or 0
            )
            if orphan_count:
                name = constraint.name or f"foreign_key_{ordinal}"
                raise MySQLImportError(
                    f"MySQL 导入后外键对账失败：{table.name}.{name}"
                )


def _reset_mysql_auto_increment(connection: Connection) -> None:
    next_values: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
    with connection.begin():
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
            if not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer):
                continue
            maximum = connection.scalar(select(func.max(primary_key)))
            next_values.append(
                (table.name, 1 if maximum is None else int(maximum) + 1)
            )
    for table_name, next_value in next_values:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"ALTER TABLE {_mysql_identifier(table_name)} "
            f"AUTO_INCREMENT = {next_value}"
        )
        connection.commit()


def _invariant_state(connection: Connection) -> str:
    expected_guards = {
        (spec.table_name, spec.guard_column)
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    installed_guards = {
        (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME))
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME "
                "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
            )
        )
    }
    expected_indexes = {spec.index_name for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS}
    installed_indexes = {
        str(row.INDEX_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT DISTINCT INDEX_NAME "
                "FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND INDEX_NAME LIKE 'uq_%'"
            )
        )
        if str(row.INDEX_NAME) in expected_indexes
    }
    expected_triggers = sum(
        contract.owner == "database_trigger"
        for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    )
    installed_triggers = len(installed_mysql_invariants(connection))
    if not installed_guards and not installed_indexes and not installed_triggers:
        return "absent"
    if (
        installed_guards == expected_guards
        and installed_indexes == expected_indexes
        and installed_triggers == expected_triggers
    ):
        return "complete"
    return "partial"


def finalize_mysql_import_target(target: Engine) -> None:
    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 导入终态只能安装到 MySQL")
    with target.connect() as connection:
        state = _invariant_state(connection)
        connection.commit()
        if state == "complete":
            return
        if state == "partial":
            raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 不变量处于部分安装状态，禁止自动修复")
        try:
            install_mysql_invariants(connection)
            connection.commit()
        except SQLAlchemyError:
            connection.rollback()
            raise MySQLImportError(
                "MySQL 不变量安装失败；数据库细节已隐藏"
            ) from None
        if _invariant_state(connection) != "complete":
            raise MySQLImportError("MySQL 不变量安装后内省不完整")
        connection.commit()


def import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
    stop_after_batches: int | None = None,
) -> ImportReport:
    if batch_size <= 0:
        raise MySQLImportError("batch_size 必须大于 0")
    if stop_after_batches is not None and stop_after_batches <= 0:
        raise MySQLImportError("stop_after_batches 必须大于 0")
    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLImportError("导入目标必须是 MySQL")
    resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(source)
    source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    inserted_rows = 0
    duplicate_rows = 0
    committed_batches = 0
    try:
        source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
            source_connection,
            Base.metadata,
        )
        source_schema_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        complete_plan = ImportPlan.from_metadata(Base.metadata)
        plan = ImportPlan(
            tuple(
                table_name
                for table_name in complete_plan
                if table_name in source_columns_by_table
            )
        )
        with target.connect() as connection:
            target_schema = _target_schema(connection)
            checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-mysql-import.json"
            checkpoint, resumed = load_checkpoint(
                checkpoint_path,
                source=resolved_source,
                source_size=source_stat.st_size,
                source_sha256=source_sha256,
                plan=plan,
                format_name="sqlite-to-mysql-import-v1",
                target_identity={"target_schema": target_schema},
            )
            _assert_target_ready(
                connection,
                checkpoint=checkpoint,
                resumed=resumed,
            )
            connection.commit()
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
            connection.commit()
            try:
                table_progress: dict[str, Any] = checkpoint["tables"]
                for table_name in plan:
                    table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
                    progress = table_progress.setdefault(
                        table_name,
                        {
                            "completed": False,
                            "imported_rows": 0,
                            "last_primary_key": None,
                        },
                    )
                    if progress["completed"]:
                        continue
                    while True:
                        rows = source_batch(
                            source_connection,
                            table,
                            last_primary_key=progress["last_primary_key"],
                            batch_size=batch_size,
                            source_columns=source_columns_by_table[table_name],
                            source_schema_version=source_schema_version,
                        )
                        if not rows:
                            progress["completed"] = True
                            write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                            break
                        transaction = connection.begin()
                        try:
                            inserted, duplicates = _insert_batch(
                                connection,
                                table,
                                rows,
                            )
                            transaction.commit()
                        except SnapshotImportError:
                            transaction.rollback()
                            raise
                        except SQLAlchemyError:
                            transaction.rollback()
                            raise MySQLImportError(
                                f"MySQL 导入表 {table.name} 的批次失败；"
                                "行内容已隐藏"
                            ) from None
                        inserted_rows += inserted
                        duplicate_rows += duplicates
                        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                        progress["last_primary_key"] = rows[-1][
                            primary_key.name
                        ]
                        progress["imported_rows"] = int(
                            progress["imported_rows"]
                        ) + len(rows)
                        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                        committed_batches += 1
                        if (
                            stop_after_batches is not None
                            and committed_batches >= stop_after_batches
                        ):
                            raise InjectedImportStop(
                                f"在第 {committed_batches} 个已提交批次后注入停止"
                            )
                with connection.begin():
                    _verify_imported_row_counts(
                        connection,
                        checkpoint=checkpoint,
                        plan=plan,
                    )
            finally:
                if connection.in_transaction():
                    connection.rollback()
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1"
                )
                connection.commit()

            with connection.begin():
                _verify_no_foreign_key_orphans(connection)
            _reset_mysql_auto_increment(connection)

        finalize_mysql_import_target(target)
        checkpoint["complete"] = True
        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
        table_rows = {
            table_name: int(progress["imported_rows"])
            for table_name, progress in checkpoint["tables"].items()
        }
        return ImportReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            inserted_rows=inserted_rows,
            duplicate_rows=duplicate_rows,
            table_rows=table_rows,
            completed_tables=sum(
                1
                for progress in checkpoint["tables"].values()
                if progress["completed"]
            ),
            resumed=resumed,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        verify_source_unchanged(
            resolved_source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_sha256,
        )


def resume_mysql_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
) -> ImportReport:
    return import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=batch_size,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/mysql_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import plistlib
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import signal
import socket as socket_module
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import CompletedProcess
from typing import Any, Literal, TextIO

from sqlalchemy import URL
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from .database_capabilities import DatabaseDialectError, parse_mysql_version
from .security import protect_secret, unprotect_secret


INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT = Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD")
MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT = (
    INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / "3d66-label-system-integration" / "mysql84"
)
PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES = 120 * 1024**3
MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES = 40 * 1024**3
MYSQL_PORT = 33084
_INSTANCE_FORMAT = "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1"
_ACCOUNTS_FORMAT = "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1"


class IntegrationPathError(ValueError):
    """Raised when local MySQL files could escape the approved SSD root."""


class IntegrationCapacityError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the local integration workspace breaches its disk gate."""


class MySQLIntegrationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the isolated MySQL 8.4 instance cannot be managed safely."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLBinaries:
    mysqld: Path
    mysql: Path
    mysqladmin: Path

    @classmethod
    def for_prefix(cls, prefix: Path) -> "MySQLBinaries":
        binary_dir = prefix.expanduser().resolve(strict=False) / "bin"
        return cls(
            mysqld=binary_dir / "mysqld",
            mysql=binary_dir / "mysql",
            mysqladmin=binary_dir / "mysqladmin",
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLIntegrationPaths:
    root: Path
    datadir: Path
    socket_dir: Path
    socket: Path
    logs: Path
    checkpoints: Path
    reports: Path
    config: Path
    pid_file: Path
    accounts_file: Path
    instance_marker: Path
    port: int = MYSQL_PORT

    @classmethod
    def from_root(cls, root: Path) -> "MySQLIntegrationPaths":
        absolute_root = root.expanduser().absolute()
        return cls(
            root=absolute_root,
            datadir=absolute_root / "data",
            socket_dir=absolute_root / "socket",
            socket=absolute_root / "socket" / "mysql.sock",
            logs=absolute_root / "logs",
            checkpoints=absolute_root / "checkpoints",
            reports=absolute_root / "reports",
            config=absolute_root / "my.cnf",
            pid_file=absolute_root / "mysql.pid",
            accounts_file=absolute_root / "accounts.json",
            instance_marker=absolute_root / "instance.json",
        )

    def assert_safe(self) -> None:
        approved_volume = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.expanduser().absolute()
        approved_root = MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT.expanduser().absolute()
        if self.root != approved_root or self.root.parent.parent != approved_volume:
            raise IntegrationPathError(
                "MySQL 8.4 集成数据只能位于 WorkSSD 的项目专属 mysql84 目录"
            )
        if self.root.is_symlink():
            raise IntegrationPathError("MySQL 8.4 项目目录不得使用符号链接")
        for child in (
            self.datadir,
            self.socket_dir,
            self.logs,
            self.checkpoints,
            self.reports,
            self.config,
            self.pid_file,
            self.accounts_file,
            self.instance_marker,
        ):
            if child.is_symlink():
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录内不得使用符号链接：{child.name}"
                )
            if child.exists() and not child.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(
                self.root.resolve(strict=True)
            ):
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录子路径越界：{child.name}"
                )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLAccountReferences:
    root_user: str
    root_password_reference: str
    migration_user: str
    migration_password_reference: str
    app_user: str
    app_password_reference: str
    test_user: str
    test_password_reference: str


CommandRunner = Callable[[Sequence[str]], CompletedProcess[str]]
ProcessStarter = Callable[..., Any]


def _directory_size(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0
    total = 0
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        directory = pending.pop()
        with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
            for entry in entries:
                if entry.is_symlink():
                    raise IntegrationPathError(
                        f"MySQL 8.4 项目目录内不得使用符号链接：{entry.name}"
                    )
                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                    pending.append(Path(entry.path))
                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                    total += entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False).st_size
    return total


def integration_disk_report(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
) -> dict[str, int | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    project_used_bytes = _directory_size(paths.root)
    probe = paths.root if paths.root.exists() else paths.root.parent
    disk = shutil.disk_usage(probe)
    if project_used_bytes >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "MySQL 8.4 项目目录已达到 120 GiB 上限，禁止继续写入"
        )
    if disk.free < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "MySQL 8.4 数据盘可用空间低于 40 GiB，禁止继续写入"
        )
    return {
        "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
        "project_used_bytes": project_used_bytes,
        "project_limit_bytes": PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": disk.total,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": disk.free,
        "minimum_free_bytes": MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES,
    }


def _default_run_command(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        tuple(command),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )


def _run_checked(
    command: Sequence[str],
    run_command: CommandRunner,
) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    completed = run_command(tuple(command))
    if int(getattr(completed, "returncode", 1)) != 0:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"MySQL 8.4 命令执行失败（exit {completed.returncode}）"
        )
    return completed


def _require_binaries(binaries: MySQLBinaries) -> None:
    missing = [
        str(path)
        for path in (binaries.mysqld, binaries.mysql, binaries.mysqladmin)
        if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path, os.X_OK)
    ]
    if missing:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            "MySQL 8.4 可执行文件不完整：" + "、".join(missing)
        )


def _port_available(port: int) -> bool:
    probe = socket_module.socket(socket_module.AF_INET, socket_module.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        probe.setsockopt(socket_module.SOL_SOCKET, socket_module.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        probe.bind(("127.0.0.1", port))
    except OSError:
        return False
    finally:
        probe.close()
    return True


def _prepare_directories(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if not INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.is_dir():
        raise IntegrationPathError("WorkSSD 未挂载，禁止创建 MySQL 8.4 集成目录")
    paths.root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(paths.root, 0o700)
    for directory in (
        paths.datadir,
        paths.socket_dir,
        paths.logs,
        paths.checkpoints,
        paths.reports,
    ):
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(directory, 0o700)
    paths.assert_safe()


def _atomic_write_text(path: Path, content: str, *, mode: int = 0o600) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
        text=True,
    )
    temporary = Path(temporary_name)
    try:
        os.fchmod(descriptor, mode)
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
        os.chmod(path, mode)
    finally:
        if temporary.exists():
            temporary.unlink()


def _mysql_configuration(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        (
            "# Managed by 3d66 label-system local integration tooling.",
            "[mysqld]",
            f"datadir={paths.datadir}",
            f"socket={paths.socket}",
            f"pid-file={paths.pid_file}",
            f"log-error={paths.logs / 'mysql-error.log'}",
            "bind-address=127.0.0.1",
            f"port={paths.port}",
            "mysqlx=0",
            "skip-name-resolve=ON",
            "default-storage-engine=InnoDB",
            "transaction-isolation=READ-COMMITTED",
            "character-set-server=utf8mb4",
            "collation-server=utf8mb4_0900_as_cs",
            "default-time-zone=+00:00",
            "local-infile=0",
            "symbolic-links=0",
            "secure-file-priv=NULL",
            "innodb-flush-log-at-trx-commit=1",
            "log-bin-trust-function-creators=ON",
            "binlog-expire-logs-auto-purge=ON",
            "binlog-expire-logs-seconds=86400",
            "sql-mode=STRICT_TRANS_TABLES,ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO,NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION",
            "",
            "[client]",
            f"socket={paths.socket}",
            f"port={paths.port}",
            "default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            "",
        )
    )


def _read_json_object(path: Path, *, description: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}不存在或不是普通文件")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}无法读取") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(f"{description}必须是 JSON 对象")
    return payload


def _verified_instance_marker(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not paths.instance_marker.exists():
        return None
    marker = _read_json_object(paths.instance_marker, description="MySQL 8.4 实例标记")
    if (
        marker.get("format") != _INSTANCE_FORMAT
        or marker.get("root") != str(paths.root.resolve())
        or marker.get("port") != paths.port
        or not paths.datadir.is_dir()
        or paths.datadir.is_symlink()
    ):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 实例标记与当前目录不一致")
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(str(marker.get("version", "")))
    except DatabaseDialectError as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("已有实例不是 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x") from exc
    return marker


def _binary_version(
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    run_command: CommandRunner,
) -> str:
    completed = _run_checked((str(binaries.mysqld), "--version"), run_command)
    output = f"{completed.stdout}\n{completed.stderr}".strip()
    match = re.search(r"\b(\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:[-+][^\s]+)?)", output)
    if match is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法识别 mysqld 版本；本地实例必须使用 MySQL 8.4")
    version = match.group(1)
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(version)
    except DatabaseDialectError as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x；实际为 {version}"
        ) from exc
    if "mariadb" in output.lower():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("本地集成必须使用 Oracle MySQL 8.4.x")
    return version


def _default_filesystem_type(path: Path) -> str:
    completed = _default_run_command(
        ("/usr/sbin/diskutil", "info", "-plist", str(path))
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法识别 WorkSSD 文件系统")
    try:
        payload = plistlib.loads(completed.stdout.encode("utf-8"))
    except (plistlib.InvalidFileException, ValueError) as exc:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("无法解析 WorkSSD 文件系统信息") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息不是属性字典")
    filesystem = payload.get("FilesystemType") or payload.get("FilesystemName")
    if not isinstance(filesystem, str) or not filesystem.strip():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息缺少类型")
    return filesystem.strip().lower()


def preflight_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    require_executables: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, int | str | bool]:
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    volume = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=True)
    filesystem = filesystem_type(volume).strip().lower()
    if filesystem != "apfs":
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"MySQL 8.4 集成数据盘必须使用 APFS，当前识别为 {filesystem or '未知'}"
        )
    if require_executables:
        _require_binaries(binaries)
    version = _binary_version(binaries, run_command)
    return {
        **report,
        "filesystem": filesystem,
        "mysql_version": version,
        "local_only": True,
        "port": paths.port,
        "port_available": _port_available(paths.port),
    }


def initialize_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if _verified_instance_marker(paths) is not None:
        return
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _require_binaries(binaries)
    version = _binary_version(binaries, run_command)
    if not _port_available(paths.port):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成端口 {paths.port} 已被占用，禁止初始化 MySQL 8.4"
        )
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    if any(paths.datadir.iterdir()):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 data 目录非空但缺少实例标记，禁止覆盖")
    _atomic_write_text(paths.config, _mysql_configuration(paths))
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--initialize-insecure",
        ),
        run_command,
    )
    auto_config = paths.datadir / "auto.cnf"
    if auto_config.is_symlink() or not auto_config.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("mysqld 初始化后未生成 data/auto.cnf")
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.instance_marker,
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": _INSTANCE_FORMAT,
                "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
                "port": paths.port,
                "version": version,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            indent=2,
        )
        + "\n",
    )


def _wait_for_mysql_ready(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    timeout_seconds: float = 30.0,
) -> None:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_seconds
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if paths.pid_file.is_file() and (
            paths.socket.is_socket() or not _port_available(paths.port)
        ):
            return
        time.sleep(0.1)
    raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 未在 30 秒内就绪")


def start_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    binaries: MySQLBinaries,
    *,
    start_process: ProcessStarter = subprocess.Popen,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    _require_binaries(binaries)
    if paths.config.is_symlink() or not paths.config.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 配置文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    if paths.pid_file.exists():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件已存在，请先检查实例状态")
    start_process(
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--daemonize",
        ),
        stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        close_fds=True,
    )
    _wait_for_mysql_ready(paths)


def _default_process_command(pid: int) -> str:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        ("/bin/ps", "-p", str(pid), "-o", "command="),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    return completed.stdout.strip() if completed.returncode == 0 else ""


def _default_terminate_pid(pid: int) -> None:
    os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)


def _read_pid(paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths) -> int | None:
    if not paths.pid_file.exists():
        return None
    if paths.pid_file.is_symlink() or not paths.pid_file.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    raw_pid = paths.pid_file.read_text(encoding="ascii").strip()
    if re.fullmatch(r"[1-9][0-9]{1,9}", raw_pid) is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件内容不合法")
    return int(raw_pid)


def _is_project_mysql_process(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    pid: int,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str],
) -> bool:
    command = process_command(pid)
    return "mysqld" in command and f"--defaults-file={paths.config}" in command


def mysql_status(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str] = _default_process_command,
) -> dict[str, bool | int | str | None]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    pid = _read_pid(paths)
    running = pid is not None and _is_project_mysql_process(
        paths, pid, process_command
    )
    return {
        "running": running,
        "pid": pid if running else None,
        "root": str(paths.root),
        "socket": str(paths.socket),
        "port": paths.port,
    }


def stop_mysql(
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    process_command: Callable[[int], str] = _default_process_command,
    terminate_pid: Callable[[int], None] = _default_terminate_pid,
) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    _verified_instance_marker(paths)
    pid = _read_pid(paths)
    if pid is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 8.4 PID 文件不存在或不是普通文件")
    if not _is_project_mysql_process(paths, pid, process_command):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("PID 对应进程不属于本项目 MySQL 8.4 实例，拒绝停止")
    terminate_pid(pid)


def bootstrap_mysql_accounts(
    connection: Connection,
    paths: MySQLIntegrationPaths,
    *,
    protect_secret_fn: Callable[..., str] = protect_secret,
    password_factory: Callable[[], str] = lambda: secrets.token_urlsafe(48),
) -> MySQLAccountReferences:
    paths.assert_safe()
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户只能在 MySQL 连接上初始化")
    account_specs = (
        ("root", "mysql84-root"),
        ("labelsystem_migration", "mysql84-migration"),
        ("labelsystem_app", "mysql84-app"),
        ("labelsystem_test_admin", "mysql84-test-admin"),
    )
    passwords = {user: password_factory() for user, _account in account_specs}
    references = {
        user: protect_secret_fn(passwords[user], account=account)
        for user, account in account_specs
    }
    statements = (
        ("ALTER USER 'root'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s", "root"),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_migration'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_migration",
        ),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_app'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_app",
        ),
        (
            "CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS 'labelsystem_test_admin'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
            "labelsystem_test_admin",
        ),
    )
    for statement, user in statements:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(statement, (passwords[user],))
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `labelsystem\\_integration\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_migration'@'localhost'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON `labelsystem\\_integration\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_app'@'localhost'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `labelsystem\\_test\\_%%`.* "
        "TO 'labelsystem_test_admin'@'localhost'"
    )
    result = MySQLAccountReferences(
        root_user="root",
        root_password_reference=references["root"],
        migration_user="labelsystem_migration",
        migration_password_reference=references["labelsystem_migration"],
        app_user="labelsystem_app",
        app_password_reference=references["labelsystem_app"],
        test_user="labelsystem_test_admin",
        test_password_reference=references["labelsystem_test_admin"],
    )
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.accounts_file,
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": _ACCOUNTS_FORMAT,
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": paths.port,
                "root_user": result.root_user,
                "root_password_reference": result.root_password_reference,
                "migration_user": result.migration_user,
                "migration_password_reference": result.migration_password_reference,
                "app_user": result.app_user,
                "app_password_reference": result.app_password_reference,
                "test_user": result.test_user,
                "test_password_reference": result.test_password_reference,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            indent=2,
        )
        + "\n",
    )
    return result


def _validate_accounts_payload(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for key in payload:
        normalized = str(key).lower()
        if "password" in normalized and not normalized.endswith(
            "_password_reference"
        ):
            raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件不得包含明文密码字段")
    if payload.get("format") != _ACCOUNTS_FORMAT:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件格式不受支持")


def mysql_database_url_from_references(
    accounts_file: Path,
    *,
    role: Literal["root", "migration", "app", "test"],
    database: str | None,
) -> URL:
    resolved_file = accounts_file.expanduser().absolute()
    if resolved_file.is_symlink() or not resolved_file.is_file():
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置不存在或不是普通文件")
    paths = MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(resolved_file.parent)
    paths.assert_safe()
    if resolved_file != paths.accounts_file:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户文件路径不受支持")
    if resolved_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 != 0o600:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置权限必须为 0600")
    payload = _read_json_object(resolved_file, description="MySQL 账户配置")
    _validate_accounts_payload(payload)
    if role not in {"root", "migration", "app", "test"}:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户角色不受支持")
    expected_users = {
        "root": "root",
        "migration": "labelsystem_migration",
        "app": "labelsystem_app",
        "test": "labelsystem_test_admin",
    }
    username = payload.get(f"{role}_user")
    reference = payload.get(f"{role}_password_reference")
    if username != expected_users[role] or not isinstance(reference, str):
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置中的角色引用不完整")
    if payload.get("socket") != str(paths.socket) or payload.get("port") != paths.port:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 账户配置与本地实例地址不一致")
    if database is not None and re.fullmatch(
        r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", database
    ) is None:
        raise MySQLIntegrationError("MySQL 数据库名不合法")
    password = unprotect_secret(reference)
    return URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username=username,
        password=password,
        host="localhost",
        port=paths.port,
        database=database,
        query={"unix_socket": str(paths.socket), "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )


def _cli_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Manage the isolated 3d66 label-system MySQL 8.4 instance."
    )
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for command_name in ("preflight", "init", "start", "stop", "status"):
        command = commands.add_parser(command_name)
        command.add_argument(
            "--root",
            type=Path,
            default=MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT,
        )
        command.add_argument(
            "--mysql-prefix",
            type=Path,
            default=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/mysql@8.4"),
        )
    return parser


def cli_main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    start_process: ProcessStarter = subprocess.Popen,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    args = _cli_parser().parse_args(argv)
    paths = MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(args.root)
    binaries = MySQLBinaries.for_prefix(args.mysql_prefix)
    if args.command == "preflight":
        payload: dict[str, object] = preflight_mysql(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
    elif args.command == "init":
        preflight_mysql(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        initialize_mysql(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = {"initialized": True}
    elif args.command == "start":
        start_mysql(paths, binaries, start_process=start_process)
        payload = mysql_status(paths)
    elif args.command == "stop":
        stop_mysql(paths)
        payload = {"stopped": True}
    else:
        payload = mysql_status(paths)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "ok",
                "command": args.command,
                "root": str(paths.root.resolve()),
                **payload,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        ),
        file=output,
    )
    return 0

_BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES = frozenset(
    {
        "trg_mysql_dimension_schemas_no_self_insert",
        "trg_mysql_dimension_schemas_no_self_update",
    }
)
_EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS = frozenset(
    contract.name
    for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
)
_EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS = frozenset(
    str(constraint.name)
    for table in mysql_metadata().tables.values()
    for constraint in table.constraints
    if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
)
_MIN_FORWARD_COMPATIBLE_SOURCE_VERSION = 81


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AutoIncrementState:
    table_name: str
    primary_key_max: int | None
    next_value: int | None

    @property
    def safe(self) -> bool:
        minimum = 1 if self.primary_key_max is None else self.primary_key_max + 1
        return self.next_value is not None and self.next_value >= minimum


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLReconciliationReport:
    source: Path
    source_sha256: str
    source_quick_check: str
    source_schema_version: int | None
    target_schema: str
    target_schema_version: int | None
    target_mysql_version: str
    source_foreign_key_violations: int
    target_foreign_key_violations: dict[str, int]
    schema_facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation]
    auto_increments: dict[str, AutoIncrementState]
    generated_unique_guards: tuple[str, ...]
    generated_guard_indexes: tuple[str, ...]
    source_uri_hash_contract: bool
    installed_invariant_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_baseline_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_triggers: tuple[str, ...]
    installed_check_constraints: tuple[str, ...]
    forward_compatible_source: bool

    @property
    def data_evidence_sha256(self) -> str:
        """Canonical content evidence independent of the target schema name."""

        payload = {
            "source_sha256": self.source_sha256,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "tables": {
                name: asdict(table)
                for name, table in sorted(self.tables.items())
            },
            "source_foreign_key_violations": self.source_foreign_key_violations,
            "target_foreign_key_violations": dict(
                sorted(self.target_foreign_key_violations.items())
            ),
        }
        encoded = json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "source": str(self.source),
            "source_sha256": self.source_sha256,
            "source_quick_check": self.source_quick_check,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema": self.target_schema,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "target_mysql_version": self.target_mysql_version,
            "source_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.source_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "target_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.target_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "schema_facts": self.schema_facts,
            "tables": {
                name: {**asdict(table), "matches": table.matches}
                for name, table in self.tables.items()
            },
            "auto_increments": {
                name: {**asdict(state), "safe": state.safe}
                for name, state in self.auto_increments.items()
            },
            "generated_unique_guards": list(self.generated_unique_guards),
            "generated_guard_indexes": list(self.generated_guard_indexes),
            "source_uri_hash_contract": self.source_uri_hash_contract,
            "installed_invariant_triggers": list(
                self.installed_invariant_triggers
            ),
            "installed_baseline_triggers": list(
                self.installed_baseline_triggers
            ),
            "installed_triggers": list(self.installed_triggers),
            "installed_check_constraints": list(
                self.installed_check_constraints
            ),
            "forward_compatible_source": self.forward_compatible_source,
            "data_evidence_sha256": self.data_evidence_sha256,
        }

    def to_markdown(self, *, accepted: bool, error: str = "") -> str:
        mismatches = [
            name for name, table in self.tables.items() if not table.matches
        ]
        target_fk_total = sum(self.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
        lines = [
            "# SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告",
            "",
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}",
            f"- 源快照：`{self.source}`",
            f"- 源 SHA-256：`{self.source_sha256}`",
            f"- 目标 schema：`{self.target_schema}`",
            f"- MySQL：`{self.target_mysql_version}`",
            f"- 数据证据 SHA-256：`{self.data_evidence_sha256}`",
            f"- 表：{len(self.tables)}",
            "- 源/目标业务行："
            f"{sum(item.source_row_count for item in self.tables.values())} / "
            f"{sum(item.target_row_count for item in self.tables.values())}",
            f"- 行级不一致表：{len(mismatches)}",
            f"- SQLite 外键违规：{self.source_foreign_key_violations}",
            f"- MySQL 外键违规：{target_fk_total}",
            f"- MySQL 不变量触发器：{len(self.installed_invariant_triggers)}",
            f"- MySQL 生成列唯一约束：{len(self.generated_unique_guards)}",
            f"- Schema 版本：{self.source_schema_version} → "
            f"{self.target_schema_version}",
            "- 向上兼容旧快照："
            f"{'是' if self.forward_compatible_source else '否'}",
        ]
        if mismatches:
            lines.extend(("", "## 不一致表", ""))
            lines.extend(f"- `{name}`" for name in mismatches)
        if error:
            lines.extend(("", "## 阻断原因", "", error))
        lines.append("")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _source_rows(
    connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    table_name: str,
    column_names: tuple[str, ...],
    primary_key_name: str,
    *,
    source_columns: set[str],
    synthetic_defaults: dict[str, Any],
) -> Any:
    selected_names = [name for name in column_names if name in source_columns]
    if primary_key_name not in selected_names:
        raise ReconciliationError(
            f"SQLite 表 {table_name} 缺少主键列 {primary_key_name}"
        )
    selected = ", ".join(
        sqlite_identifier(name) for name in selected_names
    )
    cursor = connection.execute(
        f"SELECT {selected} FROM {sqlite_identifier(table_name)} "
        f"ORDER BY {sqlite_identifier(primary_key_name)}"
    )
    while rows := cursor.fetchmany(500):
        for row in rows:
            normalized = {name: row[name] for name in selected_names}
            normalized.update(synthetic_defaults)
            yield normalized


def _source_schema_version(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int | None:
    exists = connection.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master "
        "WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_migrations'"
    ).fetchone()
    if exists is None:
        return None
    value = connection.execute(
        "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
    ).fetchone()[0]
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def _expected_generated_columns(table_name: str) -> set[str]:
    generated = {
        spec.guard_column
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
        if spec.table_name == table_name
    }
    if table_name == "assets":
        generated.add("source_uri_sha256")
    return generated


def _schema_facts(
    source: sqlite3.Connection,
    target: Connection,
    *,
    source_schema_version: int | None,
    source_columns_by_table: dict[str, set[str]],
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    target_inspector = inspect(target)
    facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        source_rows = source.execute(
            f"PRAGMA table_info({sqlite_identifier(table.name)})"
        ).fetchall()
        source_columns = [str(row[1]) for row in source_rows]
        source_primary_key = [
            str(row[1])
            for row in sorted(source_rows, key=lambda row: row[5])
            if row[5]
        ]
        target_columns_info = target_inspector.get_columns(table.name)
        all_target_columns = [
            str(column["name"]) for column in target_columns_info
        ]
        expected_generated = _expected_generated_columns(table.name)
        target_columns = [
            name for name in all_target_columns if name not in expected_generated
        ]
        target_primary_key = list(
            target_inspector.get_pk_constraint(table.name).get(
                "constrained_columns"
            )
            or []
        )
        generated_facts = {
            str(column["name"]): str(column.get("computed") or "")
            for column in target_columns_info
            if str(column["name"]) in expected_generated
        }
        source_absent = table.name not in source_columns_by_table
        synthetic_defaults = {
            name: value
            for name, value in forward_compatible_column_defaults(
                source_schema_version,
                table.name,
            ).items()
            if name not in source_columns
        }
        effective_source_columns = source_columns + list(
            synthetic_defaults
        )
        compatible = (
            source_absent
            and table.name
            in forward_compatible_empty_tables(source_schema_version)
        ) or schema_contract_compatible(
            source_columns=effective_source_columns,
            target_columns=target_columns,
            source_primary_key=source_primary_key,
            target_primary_key=target_primary_key,
        )
        facts[table.name] = {
            "source_columns": source_columns,
            "effective_source_columns": effective_source_columns,
            "target_columns": target_columns,
            "target_all_columns": all_target_columns,
            "target_generated_columns": generated_facts,
            "source_primary_key": source_primary_key,
            "target_primary_key": target_primary_key,
            "source_absent": source_absent,
            "synthetic_default_columns": sorted(synthetic_defaults),
            "source_declared_types": {
                str(row[1]): str(row[2]) for row in source_rows
            },
            "target_declared_types": {
                str(column["name"]): str(column["type"])
                for column in target_columns_info
                if str(column["name"]) not in expected_generated
            },
            "compatible": compatible,
        }
    return facts


def _target_foreign_key_violations(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    violations: dict[str, int] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for ordinal, constraint in enumerate(
            sorted(
                table.foreign_key_constraints,
                key=lambda item: item.name or "",
            )
        ):
            elements = tuple(constraint.elements)
            child = table.alias(f"fk_child_{table.name}_{ordinal}")
            parent_table = elements[0].column.table
            parent = parent_table.alias(f"fk_parent_{table.name}_{ordinal}")
            join_condition = and_(
                *(
                    child.c[element.parent.name]
                    == parent.c[element.column.name]
                    for element in elements
