                )
            )
            local_values_present = and_(
                *(child.c[element.parent.name].is_not(None) for element in elements)
            )
            missing_parent = parent.c[elements[0].column.name].is_(None)
            key = (
                f"{table.name}."
                + ",".join(element.parent.name for element in elements)
                + "->"
                + f"{parent_table.name}."
                + ",".join(element.column.name for element in elements)
            )
            count = connection.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(child.outerjoin(parent, join_condition))
                .where(local_values_present, missing_parent)
            )
            violations[key] = int(count or 0)
    return violations


def _auto_increment_states(
    connection: Connection,
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation],
) -> dict[str, AutoIncrementState]:
    # MySQL 8.4 caches TABLES.AUTO_INCREMENT statistics for up to 24 hours.
    # Reconciliation needs the storage-engine value from this committed import.
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SET SESSION information_schema_stats_expiry = 0"
    )
    next_values = {
        str(row.TABLE_NAME): (
            None if row.AUTO_INCREMENT is None else int(row.AUTO_INCREMENT)
        )
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TABLE_NAME, AUTO_INCREMENT "
                "FROM information_schema.TABLES "
                "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND TABLE_TYPE='BASE TABLE'"
            )
        )
    }
    states: dict[str, AutoIncrementState] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
        if (
            not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer)
            or table._autoincrement_column is None
        ):
            continue
        maximum = tables[table.name].target_primary_key_max
        states[table.name] = AutoIncrementState(
            table_name=table.name,
            primary_key_max=None if maximum is None else int(maximum),
            next_value=next_values.get(table.name),
        )
    return states


def _index_inventory(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[bool, tuple[str, ...]]]:
    rows = connection.execute(
        text(
            "SELECT TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME, NON_UNIQUE, SEQ_IN_INDEX, "
            "COLUMN_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() "
            "ORDER BY TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME, SEQ_IN_INDEX"
        )
    )
    grouped: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[bool, list[str]]] = {}
    for row in rows:
        key = (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.INDEX_NAME))
        unique, columns = grouped.setdefault(key, (not bool(row.NON_UNIQUE), []))
        columns.append(str(row.COLUMN_NAME))
        grouped[key] = (unique, columns)
    return {
        key: (unique, tuple(columns))
        for key, (unique, columns) in grouped.items()
    }


def _normalized_generation_expression(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.lower().replace("_utf8mb4", "")
    return re.sub(r"[\\`()\s]", "", normalized)


def _structure_inventory(connection: Connection) -> dict[str, Any]:
    generated_rows = connection.execute(
        text(
            "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME, GENERATION_EXPRESSION "
            "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=DATABASE() AND GENERATION_EXPRESSION <> ''"
        )
    )
    generated = {
        (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME)): str(
            row.GENERATION_EXPRESSION
        )
        for row in generated_rows
    }
    indexes = _index_inventory(connection)
    installed_guards = tuple(
        sorted(
            f"{spec.table_name}.{spec.guard_column}"
            for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
            if _normalized_generation_expression(
                generated.get((spec.table_name, spec.guard_column), "")
            )
            == _normalized_generation_expression(
                f"CASE WHEN {spec.predicate} THEN 1 ELSE NULL END"
            )
        )
    )
    installed_guard_indexes = tuple(
        sorted(
            spec.index_name
            for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
            if indexes.get((spec.table_name, spec.index_name))
            == (True, (*spec.key_columns, spec.guard_column))
        )
    )
    source_hash_generated = _normalized_generation_expression(
        generated.get(("assets", "source_uri_sha256"), "")
    ) == _normalized_generation_expression("UNHEX(SHA2(source_uri, 256))")
    source_hash_index = indexes.get(
        ("assets", "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category")
    )
    source_uri_hash_contract = source_hash_generated and source_hash_index == (
        True,
        ("source_uri_sha256", "category_key"),
    )
    trigger_inventory = tuple(
        str(row.TRIGGER_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT TRIGGER_NAME FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
                "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA=DATABASE() ORDER BY TRIGGER_NAME"
            )
        )
    )
    check_constraints = tuple(
        str(row.CONSTRAINT_NAME)
        for row in connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME FROM information_schema.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS "
                "WHERE CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA=DATABASE() ORDER BY CONSTRAINT_NAME"
            )
        )
    )
    return {
        "generated_unique_guards": installed_guards,
        "generated_guard_indexes": installed_guard_indexes,
        "source_uri_hash_contract": source_uri_hash_contract,
        "installed_invariant_triggers": tuple(
            sorted(set(trigger_inventory) & _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS)
        ),
        "installed_baseline_triggers": tuple(
            sorted(set(trigger_inventory) & _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES)
        ),
        "installed_triggers": trigger_inventory,
        "installed_check_constraints": check_constraints,
    }


def reconcile_mysql_databases(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
) -> MySQLReconciliationReport:
    """Stream and compare every mapped row without mutating either database."""

    from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401

    if target.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise ReconciliationError("对账目标必须是 MySQL")
    try:
        resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(
            source
        )
        source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    except SnapshotImportError as exc:
        raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None
    try:
        quick_check_rows = source_connection.execute(
            "PRAGMA quick_check"
        ).fetchall()
        source_quick_check = ",".join(str(row[0]) for row in quick_check_rows)
        source_fk_violations = sum(
            1 for _row in source_connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check")
        )
        source_version = _source_schema_version(source_connection)
        source_compatibility_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        try:
            source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
                source_connection,
                Base.metadata,
            )
        except SnapshotImportError as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None
        reconciled_tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation] = {}
        with target.connect() as target_connection:
            health = assert_mysql_runtime_contract(target_connection)
            target_schema_value = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT DATABASE()")
            )
            target_schema = str(target_schema_value or "").strip()
            if not target_schema:
                raise ReconciliationError("MySQL 对账目标未选择 schema")
            schema_facts = _schema_facts(
                source_connection,
                target_connection,
                source_schema_version=source_compatibility_version,
                source_columns_by_table=source_columns_by_table,
            )
            for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
                columns = tuple(table.columns)
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                source_columns = source_columns_by_table.get(table.name)
                synthetic_defaults = {
                    name: value
                    for name, value in forward_compatible_column_defaults(
                        source_compatibility_version,
                        table.name,
                    ).items()
                    if source_columns is not None and name not in source_columns
                }
                source_rows = (
                    ()
                    if source_columns is None
                    else _source_rows(
                        source_connection,
                        table.name,
                        tuple(column.name for column in columns),
                        primary_key.name,
                        source_columns=source_columns,
                        synthetic_defaults=synthetic_defaults,
                    )
                )
                source_values = table_digest(
                    source_rows,
                    table_name=table.name,
                    columns=columns,
                    primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                )
                target_result = target_connection.execution_options(
                    stream_results=True,
                    yield_per=500,
                ).execute(select(table).order_by(primary_key))
                try:
                    target_values = table_digest(
                        target_result.mappings(),
                        table_name=table.name,
                        columns=columns,
                        primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                    )
                finally:
                    target_result.close()
                reconciled_tables[table.name] = TableReconciliation(
                    table_name=table.name,
                    source_row_count=source_values[0],
                    target_row_count=target_values[0],
                    source_primary_key_min=source_values[1],
                    source_primary_key_max=source_values[2],
                    target_primary_key_min=target_values[1],
                    target_primary_key_max=target_values[2],
                    source_null_counts=source_values[3],
                    target_null_counts=target_values[3],
                    source_hash=source_values[4],
                    target_hash=target_values[4],
                )
            target_fk_violations = _target_foreign_key_violations(
                target_connection
            )
            auto_increments = _auto_increment_states(
                target_connection,
                reconciled_tables,
            )
            target_schema_version = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations")
            )
            mysql_version = health.server_version
            structure = _structure_inventory(target_connection)
        return MySQLReconciliationReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            source_quick_check=source_quick_check,
            source_schema_version=source_version,
            target_schema=target_schema,
            target_schema_version=(
                None
                if target_schema_version is None
                else int(target_schema_version)
            ),
            target_mysql_version=mysql_version,
            source_foreign_key_violations=source_fk_violations,
            target_foreign_key_violations=target_fk_violations,
            schema_facts=schema_facts,
            tables=reconciled_tables,
            auto_increments=auto_increments,
            forward_compatible_source=(
                source_version is not None
                and target_schema_version is not None
                and _MIN_FORWARD_COMPATIBLE_SOURCE_VERSION
                <= source_version
                < int(target_schema_version)
                and int(target_schema_version)
                == max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
                and all(
                    fact["compatible"] for fact in schema_facts.values()
                )
            ),
            **structure,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        try:
            verify_source_unchanged(
                resolved_source,
                initial_stat=source_stat,
                initial_sha256=source_sha256,
            )
        except SnapshotImportError as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(str(exc)) from None


def assert_mysql_critical_invariants(
    report: MySQLReconciliationReport,
) -> None:
    failures: list[str] = []
    if report.source_quick_check != "ok":
        failures.append(
            f"SQLite quick_check={report.source_quick_check or 'empty'}"
        )
    if report.source_foreign_key_violations:
        failures.append(
            "SQLite foreign_key_check="
            f"{report.source_foreign_key_violations}"
        )
    target_fk_total = sum(report.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
    if target_fk_total:
        failures.append(f"MySQL foreign_key_violations={target_fk_total}")
    expected_tables = set(Base.metadata.tables)
    actual_tables = set(report.tables)
    if actual_tables != expected_tables:
        failures.append(
            "mapped_tables="
            f"{len(actual_tables)}/{len(expected_tables)}"
        )
    incompatible_schema = [
        name
        for name, fact in report.schema_facts.items()
        if not fact["compatible"]
    ]
    if incompatible_schema:
        failures.append(
            "schema=" + ",".join(sorted(incompatible_schema)[:10])
        )
    mismatched_tables = [
        name for name, table in report.tables.items() if not table.matches
    ]
    if mismatched_tables:
        failures.append(
            "tables=" + ",".join(sorted(mismatched_tables)[:10])
        )
    missing_critical = CRITICAL_TABLES - actual_tables
    if missing_critical:
        failures.append(
            "critical_missing=" + ",".join(sorted(missing_critical))
        )
    critical_mismatches = [
        name
        for name in CRITICAL_TABLES & actual_tables
        if not report.tables[name].matches
    ]
    if critical_mismatches:
        failures.append(
            "critical=" + ",".join(sorted(critical_mismatches))
        )
    expected_schema_version = max(
        migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS
    )
    if report.target_schema_version != expected_schema_version:
        failures.append(
            "schema_version="
            f"{report.target_schema_version}/{expected_schema_version}"
        )
    if (
        report.source_schema_version is not None
        and report.source_schema_version != report.target_schema_version
        and not report.forward_compatible_source
    ):
        failures.append(
            "source_schema_version="
            f"{report.source_schema_version}->{report.target_schema_version}"
        )
    try:
        parse_mysql_version(report.target_mysql_version)
    except ValueError:
        failures.append(f"mysql_version={report.target_mysql_version}")
    expected_guards = {
        f"{spec.table_name}.{spec.guard_column}"
        for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    if set(report.generated_unique_guards) != expected_guards:
        failures.append(
            f"generated_guards={len(report.generated_unique_guards)}/"
            f"{len(expected_guards)}"
        )
    expected_guard_indexes = {
        spec.index_name for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS
    }
    if set(report.generated_guard_indexes) != expected_guard_indexes:
        failures.append(
            f"guard_indexes={len(report.generated_guard_indexes)}/"
            f"{len(expected_guard_indexes)}"
        )
    if not report.source_uri_hash_contract:
        failures.append("source_uri_hash_contract=missing")
    if set(report.installed_invariant_triggers) != _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS:
        failures.append(
            f"invariant_triggers={len(report.installed_invariant_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(_EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS)}"
        )
    if set(report.installed_baseline_triggers) != _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES:
        failures.append(
            f"baseline_triggers={len(report.installed_baseline_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(_BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES)}"
        )
    expected_all_triggers = _EXPECTED_INVARIANT_TRIGGERS | _BASELINE_TRIGGER_NAMES
    if set(report.installed_triggers) != expected_all_triggers:
        failures.append(
            f"all_triggers={len(report.installed_triggers)}/"
            f"{len(expected_all_triggers)}"
        )
    if set(report.installed_check_constraints) != _EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS:
        failures.append(
            f"check_constraints={len(report.installed_check_constraints)}/"
            f"{len(_EXPECTED_CHECK_CONSTRAINTS)}"
        )
    stale_auto_increments = [
        name for name, state in report.auto_increments.items() if not state.safe
    ]
    if stale_auto_increments:
        failures.append(
            "auto_increment="
            + ",".join(sorted(stale_auto_increments)[:10])
        )
    if failures:
        raise ReconciliationError("数据库对账未通过：" + "；".join(failures))

===== FILE backend/app/mysql_schema_clone.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import BinaryIO
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import URL
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection


class MySQLSchemaCloneError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when an isolated MySQL schema clone cannot be proven safe."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CandidateIdentityNames:
    app_username: str
    migration_username: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, repr=False)
class CandidateDatabaseAccounts:
    app_username: str
    app_password: str
    migration_username: str
    migration_password: str

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return (
            "CandidateDatabaseAccounts("
            f"app_username={self.app_username!r}, "
            f"migration_username={self.migration_username!r}, "
            "passwords=<hidden>)"
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles:
    app: Path
    migration: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLSchemaCloneReport:
    source_schema: str
    target_schema: str
    stripped_definers: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLTableEvidence:
    row_count: int
    checksum: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLSchemaEvidence:
    schema: str
    migration: int
    tables: dict[str, MySQLTableEvidence]
    views: tuple[str, ...]
    trigger_count: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MySQLCloneVerificationReport:
    table_count: int
    row_count: int
    evidence_hash: str


def _validated_identifier(value: str, *, description: str) -> str:
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}", value) is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(f"{description}不是安全的 MySQL 标识符")
    return value


def _evidence_payload(evidence: MySQLSchemaEvidence) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "migration": evidence.migration,
        "tables": {
            name: {
                "row_count": fact.row_count,
                "checksum": fact.checksum,
            }
            for name, fact in sorted(evidence.tables.items())
        },
        "views": list(evidence.views),
        "trigger_count": evidence.trigger_count,
    }


def assert_exact_schema_clone(
    source: MySQLSchemaEvidence,
    target: MySQLSchemaEvidence,
) -> MySQLCloneVerificationReport:
    if source.migration != target.migration:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后 migration 不一致")
    if set(source.tables) != set(target.tables):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后表集合不一致")
    for table_name in sorted(source.tables):
        if source.tables[table_name] != target.tables[table_name]:
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                f"克隆表 {table_name} 的行数或 checksum 不一致"
            )
    if source.views != target.views:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后视图集合不一致")
    if source.trigger_count != target.trigger_count:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("克隆前后触发器数量不一致")
    payload = _evidence_payload(source)
    evidence_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            payload,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            sort_keys=True,
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return MySQLCloneVerificationReport(
        table_count=len(source.tables),
        row_count=sum(item.row_count for item in source.tables.values()),
        evidence_hash=evidence_hash,
    )


def mysql_schema_evidence(
    connection: Connection,
    schema: str,
) -> MySQLSchemaEvidence:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("schema 证据只能从 MySQL 读取")
    safe_schema = _validated_identifier(schema, description="schema")
    migration = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"SELECT MAX(version) FROM `{safe_schema}`.`schema_migrations`"
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    table_rows = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT TABLE_NAME FROM information_schema.TABLES "
        "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=%s AND TABLE_TYPE='BASE TABLE' ORDER BY TABLE_NAME",
        (safe_schema,),
    ).all()
    view_rows = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT TABLE_NAME FROM information_schema.TABLES "
        "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA=%s AND TABLE_TYPE='VIEW' ORDER BY TABLE_NAME",
        (safe_schema,),
    ).all()
    trigger_count = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
            "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA=%s",
            (safe_schema,),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    tables: dict[str, MySQLTableEvidence] = {}
    for row in table_rows:
        table_name = _validated_identifier(str(row[0]), description="表名")
        row_count = int(
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{safe_schema}`.`{table_name}`"
            ).scalar_one()
        )
        checksum_row = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            f"CHECKSUM TABLE `{safe_schema}`.`{table_name}`"
        ).one()
        if checksum_row[1] is None:
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                f"表 {table_name} 无法生成 MySQL checksum"
            )
        tables[table_name] = MySQLTableEvidence(
            row_count=row_count,
            checksum=int(checksum_row[1]),
        )
    views = tuple(
        _validated_identifier(str(row[0]), description="视图名")
        for row in view_rows
    )
    return MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema=safe_schema,
        migration=migration,
        tables=tables,
        views=views,
        trigger_count=trigger_count,
    )


def candidate_identity_names(target_schema: str) -> CandidateIdentityNames:
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    suffix = hashlib.sha256(target.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
    return CandidateIdentityNames(
        app_username=f"ls_app_{suffix}",
        migration_username=f"ls_mig_{suffix}",
    )


def prepare_candidate_database(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    source_schema: str,
    target_schema: str,
    accounts: CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
) -> None:
    if connection.dialect.name != "mysql":
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("候选数据库只能在 MySQL 上创建")
    source = _validated_identifier(source_schema, description="源 schema")
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    if source == target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 与目标 schema 不得相同")
    if not target.startswith("labelsystem_docker_candidate_"):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 不属于隔离 Docker 候选范围")
    expected_names = candidate_identity_names(target)
    if (
        accounts.app_username != expected_names.app_username
        or accounts.migration_username != expected_names.migration_username
    ):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("候选数据库身份未绑定到目标 schema")

    source_defaults = connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "SELECT DEFAULT_CHARACTER_SET_NAME, DEFAULT_COLLATION_NAME "
        "FROM information_schema.SCHEMATA WHERE SCHEMA_NAME=%s",
        (source,),
    ).one_or_none()
    if source_defaults is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 不存在")
    charset, collation = (str(value) for value in source_defaults)
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]+", charset) is None or re.fullmatch(
        r"[A-Za-z0-9_]+", collation
    ) is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 字符集合同不安全")
    target_exists = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.SCHEMATA "
            "WHERE SCHEMA_NAME=%s",
            (target,),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    if target_exists:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 已存在，拒绝覆盖")
    identity_count = int(
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM mysql.user WHERE User IN (%s, %s)",
            (accounts.app_username, accounts.migration_username),
        ).scalar_one()
    )
    if identity_count:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("目标 schema 的隔离身份已存在，拒绝复用")

    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE DATABASE `{target}` CHARACTER SET {charset} COLLATE {collation}"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE USER '{accounts.app_username}'@'%%' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
        (accounts.app_password,),
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"CREATE USER '{accounts.migration_username}'@'%%' IDENTIFIED BY %s",
        (accounts.migration_password,),
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE "
        f"ON `{target}`.* TO '{accounts.app_username}'@'%%'"
    )
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        f"GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `{target}`.* "
        f"TO '{accounts.migration_username}'@'%%'"
    )


def _atomic_private_write(path: Path, content: str) -> Path:
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary_name, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
        os.chmod(path, 0o600)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary_name):
            os.unlink(temporary_name)
    return path.resolve(strict=True)


def validate_candidate_private_root(private_root: Path) -> Path:
    root = private_root.expanduser().absolute()
    if root == Path("/Volumes") or root.is_relative_to(Path("/Volumes")):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密不得写入外置卷")
    if root.is_symlink():
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密目录不得为符号链接")
    root.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    resolved = root.resolve(strict=True)
    if resolved == Path("/Volumes") or resolved.is_relative_to(Path("/Volumes")):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("数据库秘密不得经符号链接写入外置卷")
    os.chmod(resolved, 0o700)
    return resolved


def write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
    private_root: Path,
    *,
    schema: str,
    host: str,
    port: int,
    accounts: CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
) -> CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles:
    target_schema = _validated_identifier(schema, description="目标 schema")
    if not host.strip() or any(character in host for character in "\r\n"):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("运行时数据库主机不合法")
    if port < 1 or port > 65535:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("运行时数据库端口不合法")
    root = validate_candidate_private_root(private_root)

    def rendered_url(username: str, password: str) -> str:
        return URL.create(
            "mysql+pymysql",
            username=username,
            password=password,
            host=host,
            port=port,
            database=target_schema,
            query={"charset": "utf8mb4"},
        ).render_as_string(hide_password=False)

    return CandidateRuntimeDsnFiles(
        app=_atomic_private_write(
            root / "app-database-url",
            rendered_url(accounts.app_username, accounts.app_password) + "\n",
        ),
        migration=_atomic_private_write(
            root / "migration-database-url",
            rendered_url(
                accounts.migration_username,
                accounts.migration_password,
            )
            + "\n",
        ),
    )


def _mysql_option_file(path: Path, database_url: URL) -> None:
    username = database_url.username
    password = database_url.password
    if not username or password is None:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端身份不完整")
    values = (username, str(password), database_url.host or "localhost")
    if any(any(character in value for character in "\r\n") for value in values):
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端身份包含非法换行")
    lines = [
        "[client]",
        f"user={username}",
        f"password={password}",
        f"host={database_url.host or 'localhost'}",
    ]
    if database_url.port is not None:
        lines.append(f"port={database_url.port}")
    socket = database_url.query.get("unix_socket")
    if socket:
        if any(character in socket for character in "\r\n"):
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL socket 路径不合法")
        lines.extend(("protocol=socket", f"socket={socket}"))
    lines.append("default-character-set=utf8mb4")
    _atomic_private_write(path, "\n".join(lines) + "\n")


def _safe_client_error(error_file: object, *secrets_to_hide: str) -> str:
    error_file.seek(0)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    rendered = str(error_file.read())  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    for secret in secrets_to_hide:
        if secret:
            rendered = rendered.replace(secret, "<redacted>")
    return " ".join(rendered.split())[:500]


_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX = b"/*!50017 DEFINER="
_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PATTERN = re.compile(
    rb"/\*!50017 DEFINER=`[^`\r\n]{1,128}`@`[^`\r\n]{1,255}`\*/"
)


def _stream_without_mysql_definers(
    source: BinaryIO,
    target: BinaryIO,
) -> int:
    buffer = b""
    stripped = 0
    while True:
        chunk = source.read(1024 * 1024)
        reached_eof = not chunk
        buffer += chunk
        while buffer:
            start = buffer.find(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX)
            if start < 0:
                if reached_eof:
                    target.write(buffer)
                    buffer = b""
                else:
                    safe_length = max(
                        0,
                        len(buffer) - len(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX) + 1,
                    )
                    if safe_length:
                        target.write(buffer[:safe_length])
                        buffer = buffer[safe_length:]
                break
            if start:
                target.write(buffer[:start])
                buffer = buffer[start:]
            end = buffer.find(b"*/", len(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIX))
            if end < 0:
                if reached_eof:
                    raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                        "mysqldump DEFINER 片段不完整，拒绝导入"
                    )
                if len(buffer) > 512:
                    raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                        "mysqldump DEFINER 片段超出安全长度"
                    )
                break
            token = buffer[: end + 2]
            if _MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PATTERN.fullmatch(token) is None:
                target.write(buffer[:1])
                buffer = buffer[1:]
                continue
            stripped += 1
            buffer = buffer[end + 2 :]
        if reached_eof:
            break
    return stripped


def clone_schema_with_clients(
    *,
    source_url: URL,
    target_url: URL,
    source_schema: str,
    target_schema: str,
    mysqldump: Path,
    mysql: Path,
) -> MySQLSchemaCloneReport:
    source = _validated_identifier(source_schema, description="源 schema")
    target = _validated_identifier(target_schema, description="目标 schema")
    if source == target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("源 schema 与目标 schema 不得相同")
    if source_url.database != source or target_url.database != target:
        raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("客户端 URL 与 schema 合同不一致")
    for executable in (mysqldump, mysql):
        if not executable.is_file() or not os.access(executable, os.X_OK):
            raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 客户端可执行文件不完整")

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="labelsystem-mysql-clone-") as raw_dir:
        temporary_dir = Path(raw_dir)
        os.chmod(temporary_dir, 0o700)
        source_options = temporary_dir / "source-client.cnf"
        target_options = temporary_dir / "target-client.cnf"
        _mysql_option_file(source_options, source_url)
        _mysql_option_file(target_options, target_url)
        dump_command = (
            str(mysqldump),
            f"--defaults-extra-file={source_options}",
            "--single-transaction",
            "--quick",
            "--skip-lock-tables",
            "--routines",
            "--triggers",
            "--events",
            "--hex-blob",
            "--no-tablespaces",
            "--set-gtid-purged=OFF",
            "--default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            "--skip-comments",
            source,
        )
        restore_command = (
            str(mysql),
            f"--defaults-extra-file={target_options}",
            "--default-character-set=utf8mb4",
            target,
        )
        with (
            tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode="w+", encoding="utf-8") as dump_error,
            tempfile.TemporaryFile(mode="w+", encoding="utf-8") as restore_error,
        ):
            dump_process = subprocess.Popen(
                dump_command,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=dump_error,
            )
            if dump_process.stdout is None:
                dump_process.kill()
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 逻辑导出管道无法创建")
            restore_process = subprocess.Popen(
                restore_command,
                stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=restore_error,
            )
            if restore_process.stdin is None:
                dump_process.kill()
                restore_process.kill()
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError("MySQL 逻辑导入管道无法创建")
            restore_pipe_broken = False
            try:
                stripped_definers = _stream_without_mysql_definers(
                    dump_process.stdout,
                    restore_process.stdin,
                )
            except BrokenPipeError:
                restore_pipe_broken = True
                stripped_definers = 0
            finally:
                dump_process.stdout.close()
                try:
                    restore_process.stdin.close()
                except BrokenPipeError:
                    restore_pipe_broken = True
            if restore_pipe_broken and dump_process.poll() is None:
                dump_process.kill()
            restore_return_code = restore_process.wait()
            dump_return_code = dump_process.wait()
            if restore_return_code != 0:
                detail = _safe_client_error(
                    restore_error,
                    str(source_url.password or ""),
                    str(target_url.password or ""),
                )
                suffix = f"：{detail}" if detail else "；细节已隐藏"
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                    f"MySQL 目标 schema 导入失败{suffix}"
                )
            if dump_return_code != 0:
                detail = _safe_client_error(
                    dump_error,
                    str(source_url.password or ""),
                    str(target_url.password or ""),
                )
                suffix = f"：{detail}" if detail else "；细节已隐藏"
                raise MySQLSchemaCloneError(
                    f"MySQL 源 schema 逻辑导出失败{suffix}"
                )

    return MySQLSchemaCloneReport(
        source_schema=source,
        target_schema=target,
        stripped_definers=stripped_definers,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/nas_history_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import unicodedata
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .field_demand_contracts import record_asset_version
from .models import Asset, AssetVersion
from .nas_storage import normalize_nas_uri, nas_relative_path, resolve_nas_uri


class NasHistoryMigrationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkMatch:
    asset_id: int
    original_name: str
    stored_name: str
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    source_uri: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkIssue:
    asset_id: int
    stored_name: str
    reason: str
    detail: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkPlan:
    generated_at: str
    scan_roots: tuple[str, ...]
    matches: tuple[RelinkMatch, ...]
    issues: tuple[RelinkIssue, ...]
    summary: dict[str, int]
    plan_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RelinkVerification:
    plan_hash: str
    ok: bool
    cleanup_stored_names: tuple[str, ...]
    issues: tuple[RelinkIssue, ...]


def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _safe_local_path(upload_dir: Path, stored_name: str) -> Path:
    root = upload_dir.resolve(strict=True)
    candidate = upload_dir / stored_name
    if candidate.is_symlink():
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("本地原图不能是符号链接")
    resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=False)
    try:
        resolved.relative_to(root)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("本地原图路径越过存储根目录") from exc
    return resolved


def _plan_hash_payload(
    *,
    generated_at: str,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    matches: Sequence[RelinkMatch],
    issues: Sequence[RelinkIssue],
    summary: dict[str, int],
) -> str:
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "nas-history-relink-plan-v1",
        "generated_at": generated_at,
        "scan_roots": list(scan_roots),
        "matches": [asdict(item) for item in matches],
        "issues": [asdict(item) for item in issues],
        "summary": summary,
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _normalized_name(value: str) -> str:
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", value).replace("\\", "/").strip("/")


def _choose_candidate(original_name: str, candidates: Sequence[str]) -> str | None:
    if len(candidates) == 1:
        return candidates[0]
    original = _normalized_name(original_name)
    suffix_matches = [
        uri
        for uri in candidates
        if _normalized_name(str(nas_relative_path(uri))) == original
        or _normalized_name(str(nas_relative_path(uri))).endswith("/" + original)
    ]
    if len(suffix_matches) == 1:
        return suffix_matches[0]
    basename = Path(original).name
    basename_matches = [
        uri for uri in candidates if Path(str(nas_relative_path(uri))).name == basename
    ]
    return basename_matches[0] if len(basename_matches) == 1 else None


def _scan_nas_hashes(
    *,
    nas_root: Path,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    expected_by_size: dict[int, set[str]],
) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    hashes: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    visited: set[Path] = set()
    for raw_root in scan_roots:
        root_uri = normalize_nas_uri(f"nas://maps/{raw_root.strip('/')}")
        root_path = resolve_nas_uri(root_uri, nas_root)
        if not root_path.is_dir():
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"NAS 扫描目录不存在：{root_uri}")
        for current, directories, files in os.walk(root_path, followlinks=False):
            current_path = Path(current)
            directories[:] = [
                name for name in directories if not (current_path / name).is_symlink()
            ]
            for name in files:
                path = current_path / name
                if path.is_symlink() or path in visited:
                    continue
                visited.add(path)
                size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
                expected_hashes = expected_by_size.get(size_bytes)
                if not expected_hashes:
                    continue
                digest = _sha256_file(path)
                if digest not in expected_hashes:
                    continue
                relative = path.relative_to(nas_root).as_posix()
                hashes[digest].append(normalize_nas_uri(f"nas://maps/{relative}"))
    return hashes


def build_relink_plan(
    db: Session,
    *,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
    scan_roots: Sequence[str],
    generated_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> RelinkPlan:
    if not scan_roots:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("至少需要一个 NAS 扫描目录")
    assets = db.scalars(
        select(Asset)
        .where(func.coalesce(Asset.storage_backend, "local") == "local")
        .order_by(Asset.id)
    ).all()
    issues: list[RelinkIssue] = []
    eligible: list[Asset] = []
    expected_by_size: dict[int, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for asset in assets:
        try:
            local_path = _safe_local_path(upload_dir, asset.stored_name)
        except NasHistoryMigrationError as exc:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "local_path_invalid", str(exc)))
            continue
        if not local_path.is_file():
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "local_missing", "本地原图不存在"))
            continue
        if local_path.stat().st_size != asset.size_bytes or _sha256_file(local_path) != asset.sha256:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "local_hash_mismatch", "本地原图与数据库哈希不一致"))
            continue
        eligible.append(asset)
        expected_by_size[int(asset.size_bytes)].add(asset.sha256)

    nas_hashes = _scan_nas_hashes(
        nas_root=nas_root,
        scan_roots=scan_roots,
        expected_by_size=expected_by_size,
    )
    matches: list[RelinkMatch] = []
    for asset in eligible:
        candidates = sorted(set(nas_hashes.get(asset.sha256, [])))
        if not candidates:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "nas_missing", "NAS 中没有相同哈希文件"))
            continue
        source_uri = _choose_candidate(asset.original_name, candidates)
        if source_uri is None:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "nas_ambiguous", f"NAS 中存在 {len(candidates)} 个相同哈希文件"))
            continue
        occupied = db.scalar(
            select(Asset.id).where(
                Asset.source_uri == source_uri,
                Asset.category_key == asset.category_key,
                Asset.id != asset.id,
            )
        )
        if occupied is not None:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(asset.id, asset.stored_name, "nas_source_in_use", f"NAS 来源已由素材 #{occupied} 使用"))
            continue
        matches.append(
            RelinkMatch(
                asset_id=asset.id,
                original_name=asset.original_name,
                stored_name=asset.stored_name,
                sha256=asset.sha256,
                size_bytes=asset.size_bytes,
                source_uri=source_uri,
            )
        )

    counts = Counter(issue.reason for issue in issues)
    summary = {
        "local_assets": len(assets),
        "matched": len(matches),
        **{reason: counts.get(reason, 0) for reason in sorted(counts)},
    }
    generated = (generated_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)).astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    normalized_roots = tuple(_normalized_name(value) for value in scan_roots)
    plan_hash = _plan_hash_payload(
        generated_at=generated,
        scan_roots=normalized_roots,
        matches=matches,
        issues=issues,
        summary=summary,
    )
    return RelinkPlan(
        generated_at=generated,
        scan_roots=normalized_roots,
        matches=tuple(matches),
        issues=tuple(issues),
        summary=summary,
        plan_hash=plan_hash,
    )


def _validate_match_files(
    match: RelinkMatch,
    *,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
) -> str | None:
    try:
        local_path = _safe_local_path(upload_dir, match.stored_name)
        nas_path = resolve_nas_uri(match.source_uri, nas_root)
    except (NasHistoryMigrationError, ValueError) as exc:
        return str(exc)
    if not local_path.is_file():
        return "本地原图不存在"
    if local_path.stat().st_size != match.size_bytes or _sha256_file(local_path) != match.sha256:
        return "本地原图与计划哈希不一致"
    if not nas_path.is_file():
        return "NAS 原图不存在"
    if nas_path.stat().st_size != match.size_bytes or _sha256_file(nas_path) != match.sha256:
        return "NAS 原图与计划哈希不一致"
    return None


def apply_relink_plan(
    db: Session,
    *,
    plan: RelinkPlan,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
    actor: str,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    relinked = 0
    already_relinked = 0
    occurred_at = datetime.fromisoformat(plan.generated_at)
    for match in plan.matches:
        asset = db.get(Asset, match.asset_id)
        if asset is None:
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"素材 #{match.asset_id} 不存在")
        if (
            asset.storage_backend == "nas_maps"
            and asset.source_uri == match.source_uri
            and asset.sha256 == match.sha256
        ):
            already_relinked += 1
            continue
        if asset.storage_backend != "local" or asset.source_uri is not None:
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"素材 #{match.asset_id} 当前存储状态已变化")
        if (
            asset.stored_name != match.stored_name
            or asset.original_name != match.original_name
            or asset.sha256 != match.sha256
            or asset.size_bytes != match.size_bytes
        ):
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"素材 #{match.asset_id} 与迁移计划不一致")
        file_error = _validate_match_files(match, upload_dir=upload_dir, nas_root=nas_root)
        if file_error:
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(f"素材 #{match.asset_id}：{file_error}")
        occupied = db.scalar(
            select(Asset.id).where(
                Asset.source_uri == match.source_uri,
                Asset.category_key == asset.category_key,
                Asset.id != match.asset_id,
            )
        )
        if occupied is not None:
            raise NasHistoryMigrationError(
                f"素材 #{match.asset_id} 的 NAS 来源已由素材 #{occupied} 使用"
            )
        asset.storage_backend = "nas_maps"
        asset.source_uri = match.source_uri
        db.flush()
        record_asset_version(
            db,
            source_system="nas_maps",
            source_content_id=match.source_uri,
            source_version=f"sha256:{match.sha256}",
            asset=asset,
            occurred_at=occurred_at,
        )
        append_audit_event(
            db,
            category="assets",
            action="asset_storage_relinked_to_nas",
            subject_type="asset",
            subject_id=asset.id,
            actor=actor,
            payload={
                "plan_hash": plan.plan_hash,
                "storage_backend": "nas_maps",
                "source_uri": match.source_uri,
                "sha256": match.sha256,
            },
            event_key=f"asset:{asset.id}:nas-relink:{plan.plan_hash}",
        )
        relinked += 1
    return {"relinked": relinked, "already_relinked": already_relinked}


def verify_relink_plan(
    db: Session,
    *,
    plan: RelinkPlan,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
) -> RelinkVerification:
    cleanup: list[str] = []
    issues: list[RelinkIssue] = []
    for match in plan.matches:
        asset = db.get(Asset, match.asset_id)
        detail: str | None = None
        if asset is None:
            detail = "素材记录不存在"
        elif asset.storage_backend != "nas_maps" or asset.source_uri != match.source_uri:
            detail = "素材尚未切换为计划中的 NAS 引用"
        elif asset.sha256 != match.sha256:
            detail = "素材数据库哈希与计划不一致"
        else:
            file_error = _validate_match_files(
                match,
                upload_dir=upload_dir,
                nas_root=nas_root,
            )
            if file_error:
                detail = file_error
            else:
                version = db.scalar(
                    select(AssetVersion.id).where(
                        AssetVersion.asset_id == match.asset_id,
                        AssetVersion.storage_backend == "nas_maps",
                        AssetVersion.source_uri == match.source_uri,
                        AssetVersion.asset_sha256 == match.sha256,
                    )
                )
                if version is None:
                    detail = "缺少 NAS 来源资产版本记录"
        if detail:
            issues.append(RelinkIssue(match.asset_id, match.stored_name, "verification_failed", detail))
        else:
            cleanup.append(match.stored_name)
    return RelinkVerification(
        plan_hash=plan.plan_hash,
        ok=not issues,
        cleanup_stored_names=tuple(cleanup),
        issues=tuple(issues),
    )


def cleanup_verified_local_files(
    db: Session,
    *,
    plan: RelinkPlan,
    verification: RelinkVerification,
    upload_dir: Path,
    nas_root: Path,
    confirmed_plan_hash: str,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    if confirmed_plan_hash != plan.plan_hash or verification.plan_hash != plan.plan_hash:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("清理确认哈希与迁移计划不一致")
    fresh = verify_relink_plan(
        db,
        plan=plan,
        upload_dir=upload_dir,
        nas_root=nas_root,
    )
    allowed = set(fresh.cleanup_stored_names) & set(verification.cleanup_stored_names)
    deleted = 0
    for match in plan.matches:
        if match.stored_name not in allowed:
            continue
        path = _safe_local_path(upload_dir, match.stored_name)
        if path.is_file():
            path.unlink()
            deleted += 1
    return {"deleted": deleted, "preserved": len(plan.matches) - deleted}


def relink_plan_payload(plan: RelinkPlan) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "nas-history-relink-plan-v1",
        "generated_at": plan.generated_at,
        "scan_roots": list(plan.scan_roots),
        "matches": [asdict(item) for item in plan.matches],
        "issues": [asdict(item) for item in plan.issues],
        "summary": plan.summary,
        "plan_hash": plan.plan_hash,
    }


def relink_plan_from_payload(payload: dict[str, object]) -> RelinkPlan:
    if payload.get("schema_version") != "nas-history-relink-plan-v1":
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("迁移计划版本不受支持")
    generated_at = str(payload["generated_at"])
    scan_roots = tuple(str(value) for value in payload["scan_roots"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    matches = tuple(RelinkMatch(**item) for item in payload["matches"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    issues = tuple(RelinkIssue(**item) for item in payload["issues"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    summary = {str(key): int(value) for key, value in dict(payload["summary"]).items()}  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    expected_hash = _plan_hash_payload(
        generated_at=generated_at,
        scan_roots=scan_roots,
        matches=matches,
        issues=issues,
        summary=summary,
    )
    if payload.get("plan_hash") != expected_hash:
        raise NasHistoryMigrationError("迁移计划内容哈希校验失败")
    return RelinkPlan(
        generated_at=generated_at,
        scan_roots=scan_roots,
        matches=matches,
        issues=issues,
        summary=summary,
        plan_hash=expected_hash,
    )


def relink_verification_payload(
    plan: RelinkPlan,
    verification: RelinkVerification,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    eligible_names = set(verification.cleanup_stored_names)
    cleanup_manifest = [
        asdict(match) for match in plan.matches if match.stored_name in eligible_names
    ]
    cleanup_manifest_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(cleanup_manifest).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return {
        "schema_version": "nas-history-relink-verification-v1",
        "plan_hash": verification.plan_hash,
        "ok": verification.ok,
        "cleanup_stored_names": list(verification.cleanup_stored_names),
        "cleanup_count": len(cleanup_manifest),
        "cleanup_total_bytes": sum(int(item["size_bytes"]) for item in cleanup_manifest),
        "cleanup_manifest_sha256": cleanup_manifest_sha256,
        "issues": [asdict(item) for item in verification.issues],
    }

===== FILE backend/app/nas_storage.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import mimetypes
import re
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

from PIL import Image, UnidentifiedImageError


NAS_SCHEME = "nas"
NAS_SHARE = "maps"
NAS_HOSTS = frozenset({"nas", "192.168.1.51"})
NAS_MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
NAS_IMAGE_MIME_TYPES = frozenset(
    {"image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif"}
)


class NasStorageError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NasFileInfo:
    uri: str
    path: Path
    original_name: str
    mime_type: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int | None
    height: int | None
    sha256: str


def _safe_relative_parts(value: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    decoded = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", unquote(value)).replace("\\", "/")
    if "\x00" in decoded:
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 路径包含非法字符")
    path = PurePosixPath(decoded)
    if path.is_absolute():
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 共享内路径必须是相对路径")
    parts = tuple(part for part in path.parts if part not in {"", "."})
    if not parts or any(part == ".." for part in parts):
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 路径不能为空或跨越共享根目录")
    if any("/" in part or "\\" in part for part in parts):
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 路径包含非法分隔符")
    return parts


def normalize_nas_uri(value: str) -> str:
    raw = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", str(value or "").strip())
    if not raw:
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 路径不能为空")

    if raw.lower().startswith(f"{NAS_SCHEME}://"):
        parsed = urlsplit(raw)
        if parsed.scheme.lower() != NAS_SCHEME or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS URI 格式无效")
        if parsed.netloc.lower() != NAS_SHARE:
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_SHARE_NOT_ALLOWED", "只允许 maps 共享")
        relative = parsed.path.lstrip("/")
    else:
        slash_path = raw.replace("\\", "/")
        if not slash_path.startswith("//"):
            raise NasStorageError(
                "NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS 路径必须是受支持的 UNC 或 nas:// URI"
            )
        parts = [part for part in slash_path[2:].split("/") if part]
        if len(parts) < 3:
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_PATH_INVALID", "NAS UNC 路径缺少文件位置")
        host, share = parts[0].lower(), parts[1].lower()
        if host not in NAS_HOSTS:
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_HOST_NOT_ALLOWED", "NAS 主机不在允许列表")
        if share != NAS_SHARE:
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_SHARE_NOT_ALLOWED", "只允许 maps 共享")
        relative = "/".join(parts[2:])

    safe_parts = _safe_relative_parts(relative)
    return f"{NAS_SCHEME}://{NAS_SHARE}/" + "/".join(safe_parts)


def nas_relative_path(uri: str) -> PurePosixPath:
    normalized = normalize_nas_uri(uri)
    parsed = urlsplit(normalized)
    return PurePosixPath(*_safe_relative_parts(parsed.path.lstrip("/")))


def resolve_nas_uri(uri: str, root: Path) -> Path:
    root_path = Path(root).expanduser()
    if not root_path.is_dir():
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_MOUNT_UNAVAILABLE", "NAS 只读挂载不可用")
    if root_path.is_symlink():
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_MOUNT_INVALID", "NAS 挂载根目录不能是符号链接")
    resolved_root = root_path.resolve(strict=True)
    relative = nas_relative_path(uri)
    candidate = root_path.joinpath(*relative.parts)

    current = root_path
    for part in relative.parts:
        current = current / part
        if current.is_symlink():
            raise NasStorageError("NAS_SYMLINK_REJECTED", "NAS 路径不能经过符号链接")

    resolved_candidate = candidate.resolve(strict=False)
    try:
        resolved_candidate.relative_to(resolved_root)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise NasStorageError(
            "NAS_PATH_ESCAPE", "NAS 路径超出只读共享根目录"
        ) from exc
    return resolved_candidate


def _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


# 同一个 NAS 文件在一次任务里会被反复校验：resolve_asset_path 每次调用都算一遍
# （锚图按锚点逐个调用），调用方拿到 path 后往往又自己算一遍。跨 SMB 全量重读
# 一张图的代价远高于 stat，因此用 (路径, mtime_ns, 大小) 做进程内缓存——
# 文件被替换时这三者必变，不会拿到过期摘要。
_DIGEST_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 512
_digest_cache: dict[tuple[str, int, int], str] = {}


def sha256_file_cached(path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        stat_result = path.stat()
        key = (str(path), stat_result.st_mtime_ns, stat_result.st_size)
    except OSError:
        # stat 失败时不缓存，让下面的读取抛出真实错误。
        return _sha256_file(path)
    cached = _digest_cache.get(key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    digest = _sha256_file(path)
    if len(_digest_cache) >= _DIGEST_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES:
        # 简单的 FIFO 淘汰，避免长驻 worker 无界增长。
        for stale_key in list(_digest_cache)[: _DIGEST_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES // 4]:
            _digest_cache.pop(stale_key, None)
    _digest_cache[key] = digest
    return digest


def inspect_nas_file(uri: str, root: Path) -> NasFileInfo:
    normalized = normalize_nas_uri(uri)
    path = resolve_nas_uri(normalized, root)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_FILE_MISSING", "NAS 素材文件不存在")
    size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
    if size_bytes < 1 or size_bytes > NAS_MAX_FILE_BYTES:
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_FILE_SIZE_INVALID", "NAS 素材为空或超过 25MB")

    width: int | None = None
    height: int | None = None
    try:
        with Image.open(path) as image:
            image.verify()
        with Image.open(path) as image:
            width, height = image.size
            mime_type = Image.MIME.get(image.format or "", "")
    except (OSError, UnidentifiedImageError):
        if path.read_bytes()[:5] == b"%PDF-":
            mime_type = "application/pdf"
        else:
            raise NasStorageError(
                "NAS_FILE_TYPE_INVALID", "NAS 素材不是受支持的图片或 PDF"
            ) from None
    if mime_type not in NAS_IMAGE_MIME_TYPES | {"application/pdf"}:
        guessed = mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or mime_type
        raise NasStorageError(
            "NAS_FILE_TYPE_INVALID", f"NAS 素材类型不受支持：{guessed}"
        )
    return NasFileInfo(
        uri=normalized,
        path=path,
        original_name=path.name,
        mime_type=mime_type,
        size_bytes=size_bytes,
        width=width,
        height=height,
        sha256=_sha256_file(path),
    )


def resolve_asset_path(asset: Any, settings: Any) -> Path:
    backend = str(getattr(asset, "storage_backend", "local") or "local")
    if backend == "local":
        return Path(settings.upload_dir) / str(asset.stored_name)
    if backend != "nas_maps":
        raise NasStorageError("ASSET_STORAGE_UNSUPPORTED", "素材存储后端不受支持")
    source_uri = getattr(asset, "source_uri", None)
    if not isinstance(source_uri, str) or not source_uri:
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_SOURCE_URI_MISSING", "NAS 素材缺少来源 URI")
    root = getattr(settings, "nas_maps_root", None)
    if root is None:
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_MOUNT_UNAVAILABLE", "NAS 只读挂载未配置")
    path = resolve_nas_uri(source_uri, Path(root))
    if not path.is_file():
        expected_sha256 = str(getattr(asset, "sha256", "") or "").lower()
        fallback_root_value = getattr(
            settings,
            "nas_history_fallback_root",
            None,
        )
        if (
            fallback_root_value is not None
            and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", expected_sha256) is not None
        ):
            fallback_root = Path(fallback_root_value).expanduser()
            if fallback_root.is_symlink() or not fallback_root.is_dir():
                raise NasStorageError(
                    "NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_INVALID",
                    "NAS 历史回退目录无效",
                )
            resolved_fallback_root = fallback_root.resolve(strict=True)
            fallback = fallback_root / expected_sha256
            if fallback.is_symlink():
                raise NasStorageError(
                    "NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_INVALID",
                    "NAS 历史回退文件不能是符号链接",
                )
            if fallback.is_file():
                resolved_fallback = fallback.resolve(strict=True)
                try:
                    resolved_fallback.relative_to(resolved_fallback_root)
                except ValueError as exc:
                    raise NasStorageError(
                        "NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_INVALID",
                        "NAS 历史回退文件超出受控目录",
                    ) from exc
                if sha256_file_cached(resolved_fallback) != expected_sha256:
                    raise NasStorageError(
                        "NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_HASH_MISMATCH",
                        "NAS 历史回退文件哈希不一致",
                    )
                return resolved_fallback
        raise NasStorageError("NAS_FILE_MISSING", "NAS 素材文件不存在")
    expected_sha256 = str(getattr(asset, "sha256", "") or "").lower()
    if expected_sha256 and sha256_file_cached(path) != expected_sha256:
        raise NasStorageError(
            "NAS_HASH_MISMATCH",
            "NAS 素材哈希与导入时记录不一致",
        )
    return path

===== FILE backend/app/native_acceptance_cohort.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from fractions import Fraction
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .baseline_truth import BaselineTruthError, resolve_effective_baseline_truth
from .models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, WorkflowVersion
from .nas_storage import (
    NasStorageError,
    normalize_nas_uri,
    resolve_nas_uri,
    sha256_file_cached,
)
from .native_source_run_discovery import SourceRunEvidence


_SHA256 = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")


class AcceptanceCohortError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CohortCandidate:
    item_id: int
    asset_id: int
    asset_sha256: str
    storage_backend: str
    source_uri: str | None
    stored_name: str | None
    truth_revision: int
    truth_hash: str
    truth_contract_key: str
    stratum: str
    baseline_set_id: int
    inclusion_reason: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AcceptanceCohort:
    category_key: str
    source_run_id: int
    source_baseline_set_id: int
    execution_baseline_set_id: int
    source_workflow_version_id: int
    source_workflow_version: str
    source_workflow_hash: str
    items: tuple[CohortCandidate, ...]
    cohort_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AcceptanceSupportAsset:
    asset_id: int
    asset_sha256: str
    storage_backend: str
    source_uri: str | None
    stored_name: str | None
    mime_type: str
    role: str


def _truth_stratum(expected_level: str | None, workflow_input: dict[str, object], contract_key: str) -> str:
    source = workflow_input.get("source")
    is_single = workflow_input.get("is_single")
    if source in {"domestic", "overseas"} and is_single in {0, 1, False, True}:
        return f"{source}:{'single' if int(is_single) == 1 else 'whole'}"
    if expected_level is not None:
        return expected_level
    return contract_key


def load_cohort_candidates(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source: SourceRunEvidence,
    baseline_set_id: int | None = None,
) -> list[CohortCandidate]:
    selected_set_id = baseline_set_id or source.baseline_set_id
    baseline_set = db.get(BaselineSet, selected_set_id)
    if baseline_set is None or baseline_set.category_key != source.category_key:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_baseline_set_invalid", "验收素材集不存在或类目不匹配"
        )
    rows = db.execute(
        select(BaselineSetItem, Asset)
        .join(Asset, Asset.id == BaselineSetItem.asset_id)
        .where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id == selected_set_id)
        .order_by(BaselineSetItem.id)
    ).all()
    source_item_ids = set(source.item_ids)
    if baseline_set_id is None:
        rows = [(item, asset) for item, asset in rows if item.id in source_item_ids]
    candidates: list[CohortCandidate] = []
    for item, asset in rows:
        try:
            truth = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
        except BaselineTruthError as exc:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_truth_missing", "验收素材缺少有效人工真值"
            ) from exc
        try:
            json.loads(item.asset_snapshot_json or "{}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_snapshot_invalid", "验收素材快照已损坏"
            ) from exc
        candidates.append(
            CohortCandidate(
                item_id=item.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                asset_sha256=asset.sha256,
                storage_backend=asset.storage_backend,
                source_uri=asset.source_uri,
                stored_name=asset.stored_name,
                truth_revision=truth.revision,
                truth_hash=truth.canonical_hash,
                truth_contract_key=truth.truth_contract_key,
                stratum=_truth_stratum(
                    truth.expected_level,
                    truth.workflow_input,
                    truth.truth_contract_key,
                ),
                baseline_set_id=selected_set_id,
            )
        )
    if baseline_set_id is None and {item.item_id for item in candidates} != source_item_ids:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_source_item_missing", "来源 run 的素材未完整进入验收池"
        )
    return candidates


def load_workflow_support_assets(
    db: Session,
    workflow_version_id: int,
) -> list[AcceptanceSupportAsset]:
    """Discover immutable media dependencies frozen inside one workflow."""

    workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_version_id)
    if workflow is None:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_workflow_missing", "验收工作流不存在"
        )
    try:
        nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json or "[]")
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid", "验收工作流节点快照已损坏"
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(nodes, list):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid", "验收工作流节点快照格式错误"
        )

    discovered: dict[int, AcceptanceSupportAsset] = {}
    for node in nodes:
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, dict) else {}
        snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
        snapshot = snapshot if isinstance(snapshot, dict) else {}
        if (
            config.get("module_type_key") != "anchor_calibration"
            and snapshot.get("module_type_key") != "anchor_calibration"
        ):
            continue
        content = snapshot.get("content")
        if not isinstance(content, dict):
            raw_content = snapshot.get("content_json")
            try:
                content = json.loads(raw_content) if isinstance(raw_content, str) else None
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid",
                    "验收工作流锚点快照已损坏",
                ) from exc
        if not isinstance(content, dict):
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid", "验收工作流锚点快照缺失"
            )
        anchors = content.get("anchors")
        if not isinstance(anchors, list):
            continue
        for anchor in anchors:
            if not isinstance(anchor, dict):
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid", "验收锚点身份格式错误"
                )
            asset_id = anchor.get("asset_id")
            expected_hash = str(anchor.get("sha256") or "").strip().lower()
            expected_mime = anchor.get("mime_type")
            if (
                not isinstance(asset_id, int)
                or isinstance(asset_id, bool)
                or asset_id < 1
                or _SHA256.fullmatch(expected_hash) is None
                or not isinstance(expected_mime, str)
                or not expected_mime.startswith("image/")
            ):
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_workflow_snapshot_invalid", "验收锚点身份不完整"
                )
            asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
            if asset is None:
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_support_asset_missing", "验收锚点素材不在库中"
                )
            if asset.sha256 != expected_hash or asset.mime_type != expected_mime:
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_support_asset_hash_mismatch",
                    "验收锚点素材与冻结身份不一致",
                )
            if asset.storage_backend == "nas_maps":
                try:
                    source_uri = normalize_nas_uri(str(asset.source_uri or ""))
                except NasStorageError as exc:
                    raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                        "cohort_asset_locator_invalid",
                        "验收锚点 NAS 位置不在受控共享内",
                    ) from exc
                stored_name = None
            elif asset.storage_backend == "local":
                if asset.source_uri is not None:
                    raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                        "cohort_asset_locator_invalid",
                        "本地验收锚点不能携带物理路径",
                    )
                source_uri = None
                stored_name = _safe_stored_name(asset.stored_name)
            else:
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "验收锚点存储类型不受支持"
                )
            support = AcceptanceSupportAsset(
                asset_id=asset.id,
                asset_sha256=asset.sha256,
                storage_backend=asset.storage_backend,
                source_uri=source_uri,
                stored_name=stored_name,
                mime_type=asset.mime_type,
                role="anchor",
            )
            prior = discovered.get(asset.id)
            if prior is not None and prior != support:
                raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                    "cohort_support_asset_conflict", "验收锚点素材身份冲突"
                )
            discovered[asset.id] = support
    return [discovered[key] for key in sorted(discovered)]


def _safe_stored_name(value: str | None) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "本地素材缺少受控存储名"
        )
    normalized = value.strip()
    if (
        not normalized
        or normalized in {".", ".."}
        or "/" in normalized
        or "\\" in normalized
        or Path(normalized).name != normalized
    ):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "本地素材存储名不能包含路径"
        )
    return normalized


def _normalize_candidate(candidate: CohortCandidate) -> CohortCandidate:
    if (
        candidate.item_id < 1
        or candidate.asset_id < 1
        or candidate.baseline_set_id < 1
    ):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_identity_invalid", "回归条目和素材必须有有效 ID"
        )
    asset_hash = str(candidate.asset_sha256 or "").strip().lower()
    if _SHA256.fullmatch(asset_hash) is None:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_asset_hash_invalid", "素材 SHA-256 无效"
        )
    truth_hash = str(candidate.truth_hash or "").strip().lower()
    if _SHA256.fullmatch(truth_hash) is None:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_truth_missing", "抽样条目缺少可验证的人工真值来源"
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(candidate.truth_revision, int)
        or isinstance(candidate.truth_revision, bool)
        or candidate.truth_revision < 0
        or not str(candidate.truth_contract_key or "").strip()
        or not str(candidate.stratum or "").strip()
    ):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_truth_missing", "抽样条目缺少可验证的人工真值来源"
        )
    if candidate.storage_backend == "nas_maps":
        try:
            normalized_uri = normalize_nas_uri(str(candidate.source_uri or ""))
        except NasStorageError as exc:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "NAS 素材位置不在受控共享内"
            ) from exc
        stored_name = None
    elif candidate.storage_backend == "local":
        if candidate.source_uri is not None:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "本地素材不能携带物理路径"
            )
        normalized_uri = None
        stored_name = _safe_stored_name(candidate.stored_name)
    else:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "素材存储类型不受支持"
        )
    return replace(
        candidate,
        asset_sha256=asset_hash,
        source_uri=normalized_uri,
        stored_name=stored_name,
        truth_hash=truth_hash,
        truth_contract_key=candidate.truth_contract_key.strip(),
        stratum=candidate.stratum.strip(),
    )


def _normalized_pool(pool: Sequence[CohortCandidate]) -> list[CohortCandidate]:
    normalized = [_normalize_candidate(candidate) for candidate in pool]
    identity_fields = {
        "cohort_item_duplicate": [candidate.item_id for candidate in normalized],
        "cohort_asset_duplicate": [candidate.asset_id for candidate in normalized],
        "cohort_asset_hash_duplicate": [
            candidate.asset_sha256 for candidate in normalized
        ],
    }
    for code, values in identity_fields.items():
        if len(values) != len(set(values)):
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(code, "验收池包含重复素材身份")
    return normalized


def _item_manifest(item: CohortCandidate) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "item_id": item.item_id,
        "asset_id": item.asset_id,
        "asset_sha256": item.asset_sha256,
        "storage_backend": item.storage_backend,
        "source_uri": item.source_uri,
        "stored_name": item.stored_name,
        "truth_revision": item.truth_revision,
        "truth_hash": item.truth_hash,
        "truth_contract_key": item.truth_contract_key,
        "stratum": item.stratum,
        "inclusion_reason": item.inclusion_reason,
    }


def _freeze(
    source: SourceRunEvidence, items: Sequence[CohortCandidate]
) -> AcceptanceCohort:
    execution_set_ids = {item.baseline_set_id for item in items}
    if len(execution_set_ids) != 1:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_baseline_set_mixed",
            "验收样本必须来自同一个执行基准集",
        )
    execution_baseline_set_id = next(iter(execution_set_ids))
    payload = {
        "schema_version": "native-acceptance-cohort-v2",
        "category_key": source.category_key,
        "source_run_id": source.run_id,
        "source_baseline_set_id": source.baseline_set_id,
        "execution_baseline_set_id": execution_baseline_set_id,
        "source_workflow_version_id": source.workflow_version_id,
        "source_workflow_version": source.workflow_version,
        "source_workflow_hash": source.workflow_canonical_hash,
        "items": [_item_manifest(item) for item in items],
    }
    cohort_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return AcceptanceCohort(
        category_key=source.category_key,
        source_run_id=source.run_id,
        source_baseline_set_id=source.baseline_set_id,
        execution_baseline_set_id=execution_baseline_set_id,
        source_workflow_version_id=source.workflow_version_id,
        source_workflow_version=source.workflow_version,
        source_workflow_hash=source.workflow_canonical_hash,
        items=tuple(items),
        cohort_hash=cohort_hash,
    )


def _require_size(pool: Sequence[CohortCandidate], size: int) -> None:
    if not isinstance(size, int) or isinstance(size, bool) or size < 1:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_size_invalid", "验收样本数必须是正整数"
        )
    if len(pool) < size:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_too_small", f"可用素材不足 {size} 条"
        )


def select_inspiration_cohort(
    source: SourceRunEvidence,
    *,
    pool: Sequence[CohortCandidate],
    size: int = 50,
) -> AcceptanceCohort:
    normalized = _normalized_pool(pool)
    by_item = {candidate.item_id: candidate for candidate in normalized}
    eligible: list[CohortCandidate] = []
    for item_id in source.item_ids:
        candidate = by_item.get(item_id)
        if candidate is None:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_source_item_missing", "来源 run 的素材未完整进入验收池"
            )
        eligible.append(candidate)
    _require_size(eligible, size)
    failed_ids = set(source.failed_item_ids)
    if not failed_ids.issubset(by_item):
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_source_item_missing", "来源 run 的失败素材缺失"
        )
    sort_key = lambda candidate: (candidate.asset_sha256, candidate.item_id)
    failed = sorted(
        (candidate for candidate in eligible if candidate.item_id in failed_ids),
        key=sort_key,
    )
    if len(failed) > size:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_failed_overflow", "来源失败素材数超过验收样本上限"
        )
    remaining = sorted(
        (candidate for candidate in eligible if candidate.item_id not in failed_ids),
        key=sort_key,
    )
    selected = [
        replace(candidate, inclusion_reason="source_failed_first")
        for candidate in failed
    ] + [
        replace(candidate, inclusion_reason="sha256_order")
        for candidate in remaining[: size - len(failed)]
    ]
    return _freeze(source, selected)


def select_3d_su_cohort(
    source: SourceRunEvidence,
    *,
    pool: Sequence[CohortCandidate],
    size: int = 50,
) -> AcceptanceCohort:
    normalized = _normalized_pool(pool)
    _require_size(normalized, size)
    groups: dict[str, list[CohortCandidate]] = {}
    for candidate in normalized:
        groups.setdefault(candidate.stratum, []).append(candidate)
    if len(groups) > size:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_strata_overflow", "分层数超过验收样本上限"
        )
