    for members in groups.values():
        members.sort(key=lambda candidate: (candidate.asset_sha256, candidate.item_id))

    chosen: dict[str, list[CohortCandidate]] = {
        stratum: [members[0]] for stratum, members in sorted(groups.items())
    }
    next_index = {stratum: 1 for stratum in groups}
    while sum(len(items) for items in chosen.values()) < size:
        available = [
            stratum
            for stratum in sorted(groups)
            if next_index[stratum] < len(groups[stratum])
        ]
        if not available:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_too_small", f"可用素材不足 {size} 条"
            )
        selected_stratum = min(
            available,
            key=lambda stratum: (
                Fraction(len(chosen[stratum]), len(groups[stratum])),
                stratum,
            ),
        )
        chosen[selected_stratum].append(
            groups[selected_stratum][next_index[selected_stratum]]
        )
        next_index[selected_stratum] += 1

    selected = [
        replace(candidate, inclusion_reason="proportional_stratum")
        for stratum in sorted(chosen)
        for candidate in chosen[stratum]
    ]
    return _freeze(source, selected)


def private_cohort_manifest(cohort: AcceptanceCohort) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "native-acceptance-cohort-v2",
        "category_key": cohort.category_key,
        "source_run_id": cohort.source_run_id,
        "source_baseline_set_id": cohort.source_baseline_set_id,
        "execution_baseline_set_id": cohort.execution_baseline_set_id,
        "source_workflow_version_id": cohort.source_workflow_version_id,
        "source_workflow_version": cohort.source_workflow_version,
        "source_workflow_hash": cohort.source_workflow_hash,
        "cohort_hash": cohort.cohort_hash,
        "items": [_item_manifest(item) for item in cohort.items],
    }


def write_private_cohort_manifest(
    path: Path, cohort: AcceptanceCohort
) -> None:
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
    if target.parent.is_symlink():
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_manifest_path_invalid", "验收清单目录不能是符号链接"
        )
    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL
    if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
        flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(target, flags, 0o600)
    except FileExistsError as exc:
        raise AcceptanceCohortError(
            "cohort_manifest_exists", "验收清单已存在，拒绝覆盖"
        ) from exc
    try:
        payload = (
            json.dumps(
                private_cohort_manifest(cohort),
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            )
            + "\n"
        ).encode("utf-8")
        os.write(descriptor, payload)
        os.fsync(descriptor)
    except Exception:
        target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def validate_cohort_assets(
    cohort: AcceptanceCohort,
    *,
    nas_root: Path,
    upload_root: Path,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    checked = 0
    total_bytes = 0
    for item in cohort.items:
        try:
            if item.storage_backend == "nas_maps":
                if item.source_uri is None:
                    raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                        "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "NAS 素材缺少 URI"
                    )
                path = resolve_nas_uri(item.source_uri, nas_root)
            else:
                name = _safe_stored_name(item.stored_name)
                if not upload_root.is_dir() or upload_root.is_symlink():
                    raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                        "cohort_asset_root_invalid", "本地素材根目录不可用"
                    )
                path = upload_root / name
                if path.is_symlink():
                    raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                        "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "素材不能是符号链接"
                    )
        except NasStorageError as exc:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_locator_invalid", "NAS 素材位置不安全"
            ) from exc
        if not path.is_file():
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_missing", f"验收素材 {item.asset_id} 不存在"
            )
        if sha256_file_cached(path) != item.asset_sha256:
            raise AcceptanceCohortError(
                "cohort_asset_hash_mismatch",
                f"验收素材 {item.asset_id} 内容与数据库快照不一致",
            )
        checked += 1
        total_bytes += path.stat().st_size
    return {"checked": checked, "bytes": total_bytes}

===== FILE backend/app/native_baseline_incident.py =====
"""Explicit operator maintenance, not an automatic retry or a public API.

Callers own commit/rollback. Quiesce records the exact recovery set; resume is
idempotent per child and never creates a new aggregate or changes its budget.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text, read_run_snapshot_document, prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs

import hashlib
import json

from sqlalchemy import select

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .batch_endpoint_recovery import policy
from .models import (AuditEvent, BaselineRegressionRun, ProductionRun,
                     ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation)
from .native_baseline_regression import refresh_native_baseline_run
from .workflow_model_budget import _invocation_budget_usage, fail_model_invocation
from .workflow_runtime import retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint, terminalize_runtime_run

BREAKER_ERROR = 'model_failure_streak_exhausted'
REPAIR_ERROR = 'operator_runtime_repair_interrupted'


def _contract(db, *, baseline_run_id, expected_workflow_id, expected_total, expected_cost_cap,
              incident_key, actor):
    if not incident_key or len(incident_key) > 80 or not actor or len(actor) > 80:
        raise ValueError('invalid operator contract identity')
    aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun,baseline_run_id)
    if (aggregate is None or aggregate.workflow_version_id != expected_workflow_id
            or aggregate.total != expected_total or aggregate.execution_engine != 'native_workflow'
            or json.loads(aggregate.execution_snapshot_json).get('real_execution_budget',{}).get('max_cost_micros') != expected_cost_cap):
        raise ValueError('baseline recovery contract mismatch')
    return aggregate, f'baseline:{baseline_run_id}:{incident_key}'


def _facts(db,run):
    accepted = [[a.id,a.input_hash,a.output_manifest_json,a.checkpoint_json]
        for a in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id, ProductionStepAttempt.status == 'succeeded'
        ).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id))]
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json([read_run_snapshot_text(run),run.snapshot_hash,accepted]).encode()).hexdigest()


def quiesce_baseline_incident(db, **contract):
    aggregate,key = _contract(db,**contract)
    existing = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key+':quiesce'))
    if existing:
        return json.loads(existing.payload_json)
    child_items = {item.production_run_id:item.id for item in aggregate.items if item.production_run_id is not None}
    ids = sorted(child_items)
    if len(ids) != aggregate.total:
        raise ValueError('baseline recovery contract has incomplete children')
    # Same lock order as paid dispatch: child -> attempts -> invocations -> aggregate.
    runs = db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.id.in_(ids)).order_by(
        ProductionRun.id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True)).all()
    prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, runs)
    facts = {str(run.id):_facts(db,run) for run in runs}
    aggregate_snapshot = aggregate.execution_snapshot_json
    completed = [run.id for run in runs if run.status == 'succeeded']
    for run in runs:
        runtime = read_run_snapshot_document(run)['runtime_context']
        if (run.model_execution_mode != 'real' or policy(runtime) is None
                or run.workflow_version_id != aggregate.workflow_version_id
                or run.source_type != 'baseline_regression_item' or run.source_id != child_items[run.id]
                or runtime.get('run_mode') != 'stock_regression'
                or runtime.get('baseline_run_id') != aggregate.id
                or runtime.get('baseline_item_id') != run.source_id
                or runtime.get('real_execution_budget',{}).get('max_cost_micros') != contract['expected_cost_cap']):
            raise ValueError('child frozen recovery contract mismatch')
        if run.status == 'failed' and run.error_code != BREAKER_ERROR:
            raise ValueError('unrelated failure in recovery contract')
        if run.status not in {'succeeded','failed','running','queued','retryable'}:
            raise ValueError('unexpected child state in recovery contract')
    invocations = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
        WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id.in_(ids)).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).all()
    fees = {row.id:(row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros,row.input_tokens,row.output_tokens)
            for row in invocations}
    liability = sum(_invocation_budget_usage(row)[2] for row in invocations
                    if row.status in {'reserved','succeeded','failed'})
    interrupted = []
    for run in runs:
        if run.status in {'succeeded','failed'}:
            continue
        attempts = db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id).with_for_update()).all()
        active = [a for a in attempts if a.status in {'running','leased'}]
        if len(active) != 1:
            raise ValueError('quiesce requires one unambiguous in-flight step')
        for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.status == 'reserved').with_for_update()).all():
            fail_model_invocation(db,invocation_id=row.id,error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
                error_message='用户授权修复后断点重跑；中断在途调用，保留未决费用',
                evidence={'retryable':True,'technical_error_type':'operator_interrupted',
                          'operator_incident':key,'attempt_count':1},provider_dispatched=True)
            interrupted.append(row.id)
        active[0].status = 'failed'
        active[0].last_error_code = REPAIR_ERROR
        active[0].last_error_message = '用户授权修复后断点重跑'
        terminalize_runtime_run(db,run,status='failed',error_code=REPAIR_ERROR,
                               error_message=active[0].last_error_message)
    db.flush()
    if aggregate.execution_snapshot_json != aggregate_snapshot or any(_facts(db,r) != facts[str(r.id)] for r in runs):
        raise ValueError('recovery changed frozen facts')
    if fees != {r.id:(r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.input_tokens,r.output_tokens) for r in invocations}:
        raise ValueError('recovery changed financial amounts')
    if liability != sum(_invocation_budget_usage(r)[2] for r in invocations if r.status in {'reserved','succeeded','failed'}):
        raise ValueError('recovery released unknown liability')
    refresh_native_baseline_run(aggregate)
    recoverable = [r.id for r in runs if r.status == 'failed']
    report = {'baseline_run_id':aggregate.id,'retained_completed':len(completed),
        'recoverable_run_ids':recoverable,'interrupted_invocation_ids':interrupted,
        'retained_liability_micros':liability,'cost_cap_micros':contract['expected_cost_cap'],
        'aggregate_snapshot_sha256':hashlib.sha256(aggregate_snapshot.encode()).hexdigest(),
        'preserved_facts':{str(i):facts[str(i)] for i in recoverable}}
    append_audit_event(db,category='baseline_regression',action='operator_incident_quiesce',
        subject_type='baseline_regression_run',subject_id=aggregate.id,actor=contract['actor'],
        payload=report,event_key=key+':quiesce')
    return report


def resume_baseline_incident(db, *, limit=25, **contract):
    if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= 100:
        raise ValueError('invalid bounded resume chunk')
    aggregate,key = _contract(db,**contract)
    event = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == key+':quiesce'))
    if event is None:
        raise ValueError('committed quiesce evidence required')
    report = json.loads(event.payload_json)
    if hashlib.sha256(aggregate.execution_snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest() != report['aggregate_snapshot_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('aggregate recovery contract changed')
    ids = report['recoverable_run_ids']
    event_keys = {key+f':resume:{run_id}':run_id for run_id in ids}
    done = set(db.scalars(select(AuditEvent.event_key).where(AuditEvent.event_key.in_(event_keys))).all())
    remaining = [run_id for event_key,run_id in event_keys.items() if event_key not in done]
    chunk_ids = remaining[:limit]
    # Match worker finalization: child -> aggregate -> baseline items. Lock every
    # selected child before the aggregate so concurrent recovery chunks cannot
    # invert that order on their second child.
    locked_runs = {run.id:run for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).where(
        ProductionRun.id.in_(chunk_ids)).order_by(ProductionRun.id).with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True))} if chunk_ids else {}
    prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, list(locked_runs.values()))
    if chunk_ids:
        aggregate = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.id == aggregate.id).with_for_update()
            .execution_options(populate_existing=True))
        if hashlib.sha256(aggregate.execution_snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest() != report['aggregate_snapshot_sha256']:
            raise ValueError('aggregate recovery contract changed')
    resumed = []
    for run_id in chunk_ids:
        run = locked_runs.get(run_id)
        event_key = key+f':resume:{run_id}'
        # Recheck after the child lock: concurrent operator invocations must not double retry.
        if db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.event_key == event_key)) is not None:
            continue
        if run is None or run.status != 'failed' or run.error_code not in {BREAKER_ERROR,REPAIR_ERROR}:
            raise ValueError('incident child changed; refuse unrelated retry')
        if _facts(db,run) != report['preserved_facts'][str(run_id)]:
            raise ValueError('incident child frozen facts changed')
        retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db,run_id,restart_transport_window=False)
        db.flush()
        if _facts(db,run) != report['preserved_facts'][str(run_id)]:
            raise ValueError('checkpoint retry changed accepted facts')
        append_audit_event(db,category='baseline_regression',action='operator_incident_resume',
            subject_type='production_run',subject_id=run_id,actor=contract['actor'],
            payload={'baseline_run_id':aggregate.id,'incident':key,'cost_cap_micros':contract['expected_cost_cap'],
                     'successful_checkpoints_preserved':True},event_key=event_key)
        resumed.append(run_id)
    return {'baseline_run_id':aggregate.id,'resumed':len(resumed),'resumed_run_ids':resumed,
            'remaining':len(remaining)-len(resumed),'cost_cap_micros':contract['expected_cost_cap']}

===== FILE backend/app/native_baseline_regression.py =====
"""Native workflow execution and truth metrics for stock regression."""

from __future__ import annotations
from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document

import hashlib
import json
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import replace
from threading import Lock
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .baseline_regression import (
    StockMechanismError,
    compute_level_metrics,
    resolve_stock_mechanism_selection,
    result_snapshot as legacy_result_snapshot,
)
from .baseline_truth import (
    BaselineTruthError,
    EffectiveBaselineTruth,
    resolve_effective_baseline_truth,
)
from .correction_contract import (
    correction_contract_hash,
    freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot,
)
from .models import (
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    EvaluationResult,
    ModelConfig,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

    ProductionRunRequest,
    create_production_run,
    fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs,
)
from .workflow_model_budget import (
    MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS,
    MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
    MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
    MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
    RealExecutionBudget,
    WorkflowModelBudgetError,
    historical_output_expectations,

    real_execution_node_limits,

    model_snapshot_drift_issues,
    model_snapshot_issue_summary,
